中文摘要翻译: 预测行人过街意图是自动驾驶中一项安全关键任务，然而现有方法多依赖单模态输入或稠密多模态融合策略，这些策略未能充分捕捉互补的视觉与运动学信息，同时引入了冗余的跨模态交互。本文提出ADAPT（自适应域感知行人过街Transformer），一种多模态框架，联合建模局部与全局视觉上下文以及时序运动动态，实现精确的行人过街意图预测。ADAPT处理四种空间对齐的视觉模态——包括RGB图像、局部深度图、全局语义图和全局深度图——以及自车速度、行人边界框和骨架姿态信息，通过五个专用模块：用于视觉特征提取的权重共享Swin Transformer V2骨干网络、用于分层视觉融合的跨模态引导注意力模块、用于高效时序建模的基于Mamba的运动特征编码模块、选择性保留最有信息量跨模态交互的稀疏跨模态注意力模块，以及基于Vision Transformer的时序特征融合模块用于序列级预测。在JAAD和PIE基准数据集上的大量实验表明，ADAPT在保持低计算复杂度的同时持续优于现有最先进方法。在JAAD上，本方法在JAADbeh和JAADall上分别达到0.73和0.85的AUC；在PIE上达到0.92的准确率和0.90的AUC。此外，ADAPT的推理时间仅为每样本17.23毫秒，为智能交通和自动驾驶应用提供了预测准确性与实时部署效率之间的有效平衡。

</details>


### [30] [MobileSAM2: Lightweight Segment Anything for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12297)
*Kai Jiang,Jiaxing Huang,Jingyi Zhang,Weiying Xie,Yunsong Li,Yufei Wang,Aoran Xiao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyperKD知识蒸馏方法，将SAM2大模型压缩为轻量级MobileSAM2，实现移动端图像和视频的通用分割。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAM2作为强大的视频基础模型，但参数量大、计算成本高，难以部署在手机、笔记本电脑等资源受限设备上，因此需要将其蒸馏为轻量级模型。

Method: 提出Hypergraphical Knowledge Distill (HyperKD)，引入超图概念进行知识蒸馏。包含两个模块：(1) Temporal HyperKD：构建超图显式建模和提取SAM2中的时序知识；(2) Granularity HyperKD：构建超图建模和提取多粒度知识。通过让学生模型对齐所构建的超图来实现知识蒸馏。同时通过模型架构搜索得到MobileSAM2系列模型。

Result: 在多个基准上进行了广泛实验验证，MobileSAM2在保持效率的同时展现了有竞争力的性能，并在具身AI任务上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: HyperKD能有效将SAM2的通用知识蒸馏到轻量模型中，MobileSAM2在移动设备上实现了图像和视频的通用分割能力，平衡了效率与效果。

Original Abstract: The recent large video foundation model, SAM2, enables segment anything in both images and videos, serving as a powerful base model for various applications. However, many of such use cases require to operate on resource-constrained devices like mobile phones and laptops. In this work, we aim to make SAM2 more mobile-friendly by distilling the heavyweight SAM2 into a lightweight model, facilitating segment anything in both images and videos on mobile devices. To this end, we propose Hypergraphical Knowledge Distill (HyperKD), which introduces the idea of hypergraph into knowledge distillation, aiming to effectively model and transfer SAM2's generalizable and comprehensive knowledge. HyperKD consists of Temporal HyperKD and Granularity HyperKD that construct hypergraphs to explicitly model and extract the generalizable temporal knowledge and the comprehensive multi-granularity knowledge from SAM2 respectively, which are then distilled into the lightweight student model by aligning it with the constructed hypergraphs. Besides, we present MobileSAM2, a new family of lightweight SAM2 that balances efficiency and effectiveness via searching the best model architectures with HyperKD during model size reduction. Extensive experiments validate MobileSAM2 across multiple benchmarks and show promising generalization performance on embodied AI tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 近期的大型视频基础模型SAM2能够同时在图像和视频中分割任何物体，作为多种应用的基础模型。然而，许多应用场景需要在手机、笔记本电脑等资源受限设备上运行。本文旨在通过将重量的SAM2蒸馏为轻量级模型，使其更适用于移动设备，从而在移动设备上实现图像和视频中的通用分割。为此，我们提出超图知识蒸馏（HyperKD），将超图的概念引入知识蒸馏中，以有效建模和迁移SAM2的通用且全面的知识。HyperKD包含时序超图蒸馏（Temporal HyperKD）和粒度超图蒸馏（Granularity HyperKD），分别构建超图以显式建模和提取SAM2中的通用时序知识与全面的多粒度知识，并通过让学生模型与所构建的超图对齐来实现知识蒸馏。此外，我们提出MobileSAM2——一个新的轻量级SAM2系列，通过在模型尺寸缩减过程中结合HyperKD搜索最佳模型架构，平衡了效率与效果。大量实验在多个基准上验证了MobileSAM2的性能，并在具身AI任务上展现出良好的泛化能力。

</details>


### [31] [What Does a Temporal Benchmark Score Measure? Decomposing Channel Use in Video VLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12304)
*Farrukh Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出视频问答中时间理解分数混淆了“任务问题”（问题是否真正需要时序理解）与“通道问题”（模型是从像素还是位置编码RoPE中获取时序信息）。作者提出一种无需标注的“反转-下降”诊断方法，通过反转视觉序列同时保持RoPE不变，来区分模型是依赖位置编码（位置主导型）还是依赖视觉序列（视觉序列主导型）。结果发现两个通道在不同输入上失效，因此即使分数相近的模型也不可互换。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间视频问答基准分数混淆了两种不同的问题：任务问题（问题是否真正需要时序理解）和通道问题（模型从像素还是位置编码中获取时序信息）。大部分时间分数实际上只反映单帧和答案先验，而非真正的时序理解。已有的校验方法（如帧混洗敏感度和全视频准确度）只能回答任务问题，无法区分模型依赖的时序信息通道。

Method: 提出一种无需标注的“反转-下降”（reversal-drop）诊断方法：在保持RoPE（位置编码）不变的情况下反转视觉序列，计算准确率下降值。对于有配对反转标签或标签在反转下确定性变换的任务，可以区分模型是跟随反转后的视觉内容（视觉序列主导型）还是被冲突干扰（位置主导型）。

Result: Molmo2模型是位置主导型（依赖RoPE读取正向事件的顺序），而Qwen3-VL是视觉序列主导型（依赖实际看到的视觉序列）。该区分在两种基准、多种时序任务和两个尺度上均成立。激活修补（activation patching）实验证明这是模型内部真实属性，而非冲突伪影。

Conclusion: 两个通道在不同输入上失效，因此即使分数相近的模型也不可互换——聚合分数无法反映潜在的失败模式。这一区分对于模型评估和选择具有重要意义。

Original Abstract: A score on a temporal video question answering benchmark is meant to measure that a model has temporal understanding, but it conflates two questions. 1. The task question: is the question even temporal, does it need several frames and their order? and 2. The channel question, when it does, does the model recover the order from the pixels, or read it off the positional encoding (RoPE)? Most of a temporal score answers neither, a single frame and answer priors often carry it. The field's validity checks, frame-shuffle sensitivity and the accuracy gained from the full video, speak only to the task question. We contribute a label-free screen for the channel question, the reversal-drop: the accuracy lost when the visual sequence is reversed while RoPE remains forward. It can be applied to compatible temporal benchmarks without new annotations. Paired reverse labels, or tasks whose labels transform deterministically under reversal, distinguish models that follow reversed content from those merely disrupted by the conflict. Molmo2 answers the forward event reading order off positions, while Qwen3-VL answers the reversed event it actually sees, reading visual order (comparatively). We call them position-dominant and visual-sequence-dominant. The split holds across two benchmarks and several temporal tasks at two scales, and activation patching shows it is a real internal property, not an artifact of the conflict. The distinction matters, the two channels fail on opposite inputs so two models with similar score are not interchangable, i.e. an aggregate score does not reflect potential failure modes.

中文摘要翻译: 时间视频问答基准上的分数本应衡量模型是否具备时间理解能力，但它混淆了两个问题：(1) 任务问题——问题本身是否涉及时间、是否需要多帧及其顺序？(2) 通道问题——当确实需要时序时，模型是从像素中恢复帧的顺序，还是从位置编码（RoPE）中直接读取？大多数情况下，时间分数无法回答上述任何一个问题，单帧和答案先验往往就能决定分数。该领域现有的有效性校验方法（帧混洗敏感度和全视频带来的准确率提升）仅能回答任务问题。我们针对通道问题贡献了一种无需标注的筛查方法——"反转-下降"（reversal-drop）：在保持RoPE正向不变的同时反转视觉序列，测量由此造成的准确率损失。该方法可应用于兼容的时间基准，无需额外标注。通过配对的反转标签，或标签在反转下呈确定性变换的任务，我们能够区分出那些真正跟随反转内容的模型，与那些仅仅因冲突而性能下降的模型。Molmo2通过位置信息读取正向事件的顺序，而Qwen3-VL则读取实际看到的反转事件（相对而言更依赖视觉顺序）。我们将前者称为"位置主导型"，后者称为"视觉序列主导型"。这一区分在两种基准、多种时间任务和两个尺度上均成立；激活修补实验表明，这确实是模型内部的真实属性，而非冲突引入的伪影。该区分很重要：两个通道在相反的输入上都会失效，因此即使分数相近的两个模型也不可互换——即聚合分数无法反映潜在的失败模式。

</details>


### [32] [DM-KG: A Novel Method for Boosting Spatial Cognition of Vision-Language Models in Street View Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12319)
*Xinyue Xu,Zheng Zhang,Kunyang Ma,Ge Zhu,Lianshuai Cao,Lei Wang,Zixuan Li,Yi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DM-KG，一个基于结构化的方向-度量知识图谱框架，通过显式提取单张街景图像中实体间的方向关系和度量关系，增强视觉语言模型（VLM）的空间推理能力，在空间问答任务中显著降低了距离估计和方向判断的误差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在对街景图像进行空间认知时频繁出现“空间语义幻觉”，即对物体位置、距离和方向的感知存在错误，且这些错误难以追溯和校准，严重制约了VLM在地理空间任务中的实际部署。

Method: (1) 集成全景分割与度量深度估计，鲁棒地计算逐实体的3D空间坐标；(2) 将实体对的时钟方位角和欧氏距离编码为JSON格式的知识图谱；(3) 将该知识图谱作为显式几何先验注入VLM，引导空间推理。

Result: 在公开空间问答基准上，DM-KG使距离估计的平均绝对误差（MAE）降低31.1%，方向判断的平均角误差降低65.8%，同时保持了较高的QA成功率。

Conclusion: DM-KG建立了完整、增强的推理管线，显著提升了VLM在街景场景下的空间认知能力，为开放环境中的地理视觉问答（GeoVQA）提供了一个灵活、通用且可解释的框架。

Original Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in geospatial question answering and visual scene understanding, improving their spatial cognition capability on street view imagery for complex logical reasoning has emerged as a key research priority. However, existing VLMs frequently suffer from "spatial semantic hallucinations" when perceiving object locations, distances, and directions in real-world street view scenes. Furthermore, such errors are often recalcitrant to tracing and calibration, posing a critical bottleneck for their practical deployment in geospatial tasks. To address this pressing challenge, this study proposes DM-KG (Direction-Metric Knowledge Graph), a structurally grounded spatial representation framework for street view imagery. By explicitly extracting directional and metric relationships between entities from a single 2D image, this framework enhances the spatial reasoning accuracy of VLMs through a structured knowledge graph. Specifically, we integrate panoptic segmentation with metric depth estimation to robustly compute entity-level 3D spatial coordinates. Subsequently, we encode the clock azimuths and Euclidean distances of entity pairs into a JSON-formatted knowledge graph, which is injected into the VLM as an explicit geometric prior to guide spatial reasoning. Experimental results on public spatial question-answering (QA) benchmarks demonstrate that DM-KG reduces the mean absolute error (MAE) in distance estimation by 31.1% and the mean angular error in direction judgment by 65.8%, while simultaneously maintaining a high QA success rate. By establishing a complete, augmented reasoning pipeline, this research significantly improves the spatial cognitive capabilities of VLMs in street view scenarios, thereby providing a flexible, generalized, and interpretable framework for geographic visual question answering (GeoVQA) in open environments.

中文摘要翻译: 随着视觉语言模型（VLM）在地理空间问答与视觉场景理解中的日益部署，提升其对街景图像的空间认知能力以进行复杂逻辑推理已成为关键研究重点。然而，现有VLM在感知真实世界街景场景中的物体位置、距离和方向时，频繁遭遇“空间语义幻觉”。此外，这类错误往往难以追溯和校准，成为其在空间任务中实际部署的关键瓶颈。为应对这一紧迫挑战，本研究提出DM-KG（方向-度量知识图谱），一种面向街景图像的、结构化的空间表征框架。通过从单张2D图像中显式提取实体间的方向关系和度量关系，该框架借助结构化知识图谱增强了VLM的空间推理准确性。具体而言，我们整合了全景分割与度量深度估计，以鲁棒地计算逐实体级别的3D空间坐标；随后，将实体对的时钟方位角和欧氏距离编码为JSON格式的知识图谱，并将其作为显式几何先验注入VLM，以引导空间推理。在公开空间问答基准上的实验结果表明，DM-KG使距离估计的平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了31.1%，方向判断的平均角误差降低了65.8%，同时保持了较高的问答成功率。通过建立一条完整增强的推理管线，本研究显著提升了VLM在街景场景下的空间认知能力，从而为开放环境中的地理视觉问答（GeoVQA）提供了一个灵活、通用且可解释的框架。

</details>


### [33] [ProtoPointNet: Prototype-Based Interpretable Classification of 3D Dental Point Clouds with Verifiable Spatial Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12335)
*George V. Jose,Thao Liang Chiam,Toby Hughes,Dilan Patel,Alan Brook,Lyle J. Palmer,Nikhil Cherian Kurian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoPointNet是一个基于原型网络的3D点云模型，用于牙科咬合分类。它通过结合局部几何、曲率和牙弓间距描述符，在多任务骨干网络上学习五个轴向上的原型头，在Bits2Bites数据集上达到0.724的宏F1和0.825的AUROC，且原型激活定位于解剖学合理区域，提供了透明、空间可解释的替代方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于原型的网络在3D点云和临床表面对推理中应用有限，尤其是在牙科咬合分类这一需要透明性和空间可解释性的临床任务上，现有的黑盒3D分类器缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出ProtoPointNet模型：1）将每个点编码为14维描述子，包含局部表面几何、曲率、牙弓间位移和间距；2）使用共享的多任务点云骨干网络，学习五个轴向（矢状左、矢状右、垂直、横向、中线）的特定原型头；3）通过辅助监督和编码器冻结策略从头训练原型，以应对有限的临床数据。

Result: 在Bits2Bites数据集上，平均测试宏F1为0.724，AUROC为0.825；垂直分类F1达0.828，矢状左分类F1达0.807；原型激活定位于解剖学合理区域（后磨牙和前磨牙用于反合证据，前切牙用于咬合深度证据）。

Conclusion: 原型推理可作为透明、空间可解释的方法，替代黑盒3D分类器用于牙科表面对分析，为临床表面-配对推理提供了可解释的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Prototype-based networks provide inherently interpretable classification by linking predictions to learned exemplars, but their use in 3D point clouds and clinical surface-pair reasoning remains limited. We introduce ProtoPointNet, a prototype-based model for dental occlusion classification from registered upper--lower intraoral arch pairs. Each point is encoded by a 14-dimensional descriptor combining local surface geometry, curvature, and explicit inter-arch displacement and clearance, exposing occlusal relationships to prototype matching. A shared multi-task point-cloud backbone learns axis-specific prototype heads for sagittal-left, sagittal-right, vertical, transverse, and midline classification. To support limited clinical data, we train prototypes from scratch using auxiliary supervision and encoder-freeze hand-off. On Bits2Bites, ProtoPointNet achieves mean test macro-F1 of 0.724 and AUROC of 0.825, with strongest performance on vertical (F1 0.828) and sagittal-left classification (F1 0.807). Projected prototype activations localise to anatomically plausible regions, including posterior molars and premolars for cross-bite evidence and anterior incisors for bite-depth evidence. These results support prototype-based reasoning as a transparent, spatially grounded alternative to black-box 3D classifiers for dental surface-pair analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 基于原型的网络通过将预测链接到学习到的范例，提供了本质可解释的分类，但它们在3D点云和临床表面对推理中的应用仍然有限。我们提出ProtoPointNet，一个基于原型的模型，用于从配准的上下牙弓对进行牙科咬合分类。每个点由一个14维描述子编码，该描述子结合了局部表面几何、曲率以及显式的牙弓间位移和间距，将咬合关系暴露给原型匹配。一个共享的多任务点云骨干网络学习五个轴向（矢状左、矢状右、垂直、横向和中线）分类的特定原型头。为了支持有限的临床数据，我们使用辅助监督和编码器冻结交接策略从头训练原型。在Bits2Bites上，ProtoPointNet的平均测试宏F1为0.724，AUROC为0.825，在垂直分类（F1 0.828）和矢状左分类（F1 0.807）上表现最强。投影的原型激活定位于解剖学合理区域，包括用于反合证据的后磨牙和前磨牙，以及用于咬合深度证据的前切牙。这些结果支持基于原型的推理作为牙科表面对分析中黑盒3D分类器的透明、空间可解释的替代方案。

</details>


### [34] [Filtering-out poor-quality images for data preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12352)
*Roopdeep Kaur,Gour Karmakar,Muhammad Imran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的图像质量筛选方法，通过图像质量评估指标和最优阈值过滤低质量图像，替代传统的去噪方法，在交通标志和物体识别数据集上分别达到93.8%和84.9%的平均识别准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有去噪技术（中值滤波、高斯滤波、双边滤波及CNN方法）存在两大不足：(1) CNN方法需要合适的训练数据集且需调整图像尺寸；(2) 所有滤波技术仅适用于特定类型的环境和相机噪声。为弥补这一研究空白，本文首次提出不进行去噪，而是直接过滤低质量图像的思路。

Method: 使用图像质量评估（IQA）指标对图像进行质量评分，并设定最优阈值来过滤低质量图像，同时确保保留足够数量的图像用于深度学习模型训练。

Result: 在交通标志识别数据集上平均识别准确率为93.8%，在物体识别数据集上为84.9%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该质量筛选方法在真实和模拟的交通及物体识别数据上表现优越，有望应用于自动驾驶等现实场景。

Original Abstract: Filtering noise is a fundamental part of data preparation that enhances image quality for applications such as object segmentation, detection, and recognition. Various noise reduction techniques are proposed in the literature, including the use of median, Gaussian, and bilateral filters. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have gained popularity in image denoising owing to their ability to extract complex patterns and features from data. CNNs are highly adaptable, making them effective tools for various image-denoising tasks. One drawback of CNN-based techniques is that they require an appropriate training dataset and all images to be resized. Another notable drawback of all these filtering techniques is that they work for certain types of environmental and camera noises. To bridge this research gap, in this paper, for the first time, instead of denoising, we propose an approach that filters out poor-quality images for various environmental and camera impacts. In our approach, quality is assessed using an image quality assessment metric and an optimum threshold is used to filter out poor-quality images. We also ensure that a sufficient number of images remain to develop the deep learning (DL) model. The results produced using real and simulated traffic and object recognition data demonstrate the performance supremacy of the proposed approach compared with the state-of-the-art approaches. The average recognition accuracy for our proposed approach is 93.8% for the traffic sign recognition dataset and 84.9% for the object recognition dataset. This indicates our model's potential for real-life applications such as autonomous vehicles.

中文摘要翻译: 过滤噪声是数据准备中的基本环节，可提升图像质量以适应目标分割、检测和识别等应用。文献中提出了多种降噪技术，包括使用中值滤波、高斯滤波和双边滤波。卷积神经网络（CNN）因其能够从数据中提取复杂模式和特征，在图像去噪领域广受欢迎。CNN具有高度适应性，使其成为多种图像去噪任务的有效工具。CNN技术的一个缺点是需要合适的训练数据集且所有图像需调整尺寸。所有这些滤波技术的另一个显著缺点是它们仅适用于特定类型的环境噪声和相机噪声。为弥补这一研究空白，本文首次提出了一种替代去噪的方法——针对各种环境和相机影响，过滤掉低质量图像。在我们的方法中，使用图像质量评估指标评估图像质量，并利用最优阈值过滤低质量图像。我们还确保保留足够数量的图像用于开发深度学习模型。使用真实和模拟的交通及物体识别数据所得的结果表明，与最新方法相比，所提方法在性能上具有优越性。我们的方法在交通标志识别数据集上的平均识别准确率为93.8%，在物体识别数据集上为84.9%，这表明该模型在自动驾驶等现实应用中的潜力。

</details>


### [35] [ACID: Adaptive Caching for vIDeo generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12358)
*Om Agrawal,Saurabh Agarwal,Aditya Akella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACID是一种轻量级、免训练的动态缓存加速方法，通过监测漂移信号变化率自适应切换阈值，在不显著降低质量的前提下大幅提升视频扩散模型的推理速度，在多个模型和方法上拓展了速度-质量帕累托前沿。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态缓存方法（如TeaCache、EasyCache、DiCache）使用固定阈值τ来控制漂移信号累积与模型计算跳过的权衡，导致速度与质量之间存在看似固有的折中。作者发现这一折中并非本质，而是固定阈值策略的产物——去噪过程中存在关键步骤，其漂移信号变化剧烈，在这些步骤使用低阈值、其余步骤激进缓存可在保持质量的同时大幅提升速度。

Method: 提出ACID，一个免训练、信号无关的轻量级包装器。它通过监测每个缓存方法现有漂移信号的变化率（rate of change），在低阈值和高阈值之间动态切换：对漂移信号快速变化的关键步骤应用低阈值以保护质量，对变化平缓的步骤应用高阈值以实现激进缓存。ACID无需重新训练，可直接插入现有动态缓存方法（TeaCache、EasyCache、DiCache）而不修改其核心机制。

Result: 在三种缓存方法（TeaCache、EasyCache、DiCache）和三个开源视频扩散模型（HunyuanVideo、Wan 2.1、CogVideoX）上评估，ACID持续拓展了视觉质量与推理速度的帕累托前沿。在TeaCache和HunyuanVideo上，相比无缓存基线实现最高2.16倍加速；相比保守固定阈值基线额外获得最高38%的加速，且质量退化可忽略（PSNR < 0.3 dB, SSIM < 0.01, LPIPS < 0.01）。

Conclusion: 去噪过程中的质量-速度折中并非固有，通过在不同时间步自适应调整缓存阈值可以有效突破这一折中。ACID作为一种通用的免训练包装器，能够在保持高质量的同时显著加速视频扩散模型推理，具有良好的模块化和可迁移性。

Original Abstract: Video diffusion models produce high-quality generations but remain slow at inference due to their sequential denoising procedure. Caching-based acceleration methods address this by reusing intermediate model outputs: leading dynamic approaches such as TeaCache, EasyCache, and DiCache accumulate a drift signal and skip expensive model evaluations when accumulated drift stays below a fixed threshold τ. This threshold controls an apparent tradeoff - raising it yields faster generation at the cost of visual quality, while lowering it preserves quality but sacrifices speed. We show this tradeoff is not fundamental; it is an artifact of holding τ constant throughout denoising. We identify the existence of critical steps - timesteps where the drift signal changes rapidly - and show that applying a low threshold selectively at these steps while caching aggressively elsewhere recovers most of the quality of conservative caching at substantially higher inference speeds. Building on this insight, we propose ACID, a lightweight, training-free wrapper that monitors the rate of change of each method's existing drift signal to dynamically switch between a low and a high threshold. ACID is signal-agnostic and modular: it requires no retraining and plugs directly into existing dynamic caching methods without modifying their core mechanisms. Evaluated across three caching methods (TeaCache, EasyCache, DiCache) and three open-source video diffusion models (HunyuanVideo, Wan 2.1, CogVideoX), ACID consistently expands the Pareto frontier of visual quality versus inference speed beyond what any fixed threshold achieves. In particular, on TeaCache and HunyuanVideo, ACID achieves up to 2.16x speedup over the no-caching baseline, and up to 38% additional speedup over the conservative fixed-threshold baseline with negligible (<0.3 dB PSNR, <0.01 SSIM, <0.01 LPIPS) quality degradation.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型能够生成高质量的内容，但由于其顺序去噪过程，在推理时仍然速度缓慢。基于缓存的加速方法通过重用中间模型输出解决了这一问题：领先的动态方法（如TeaCache、EasyCache和DiCache）会累积一个漂移信号，当累积漂移保持在固定阈值τ以下时，跳过昂贵的模型计算。该阈值呈现出明显的权衡——提高阈值可加速生成，但代价是视觉质量下降；降低阈值可保持质量，但牺牲了速度。我们发现这一权衡并非本质，而是去噪过程中保持τ恒定所带来的假象。我们识别出关键步骤的存在——即漂移信号快速变化的时间步——并表明在这些步骤选择性应用低阈值，同时在其他步骤激进缓存，能够在以显著更高的推理速度下恢复保守缓存的大部分质量。基于这一洞察，我们提出ACID，一种轻量级、免训练的包装器，它监测每种方法现有漂移信号的变化率，以在低阈值和高阈值之间动态切换。ACID是信号无关且模块化的：它无需重新训练，可直接插入现有的动态缓存方法中，而无需修改其核心机制。在三种缓存方法（TeaCache、EasyCache、DiCache）和三个开源视频扩散模型（HunyuanVideo、Wan 2.1、CogVideoX）上的评估表明，ACID持续地将视觉质量与推理速度的帕累托前沿扩展到任何固定阈值所能达到的范围之外。特别是在TeaCache和HunyuanVideo上，ACID相对于无缓存基线实现了最高2.16倍的加速，相对于保守的固定阈值基线额外获得了最高38%的加速，且质量退化可忽略不计（PSNR < 0.3 dB, SSIM < 0.01, LPIPS < 0.01）。

</details>


### [36] [Implicit 4D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Motion with Large Inter-Frame Displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12362)
*Seung-gyeom Kim,Areum Kim,Yongjae Yoo,Sukmin Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPIN-4DGS通过从显式收集的时空位置学习高斯属性，而非建模时间位移，解决了4D高斯抛雪球方法在快速运动和大帧间位移下的重建失败问题，使用轻量前馈网络预测属性以避免内存开销，在CMU Panoptic数据集上取得了更优的保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D高斯抛雪球方法在快速运动和大帧间位移场景下表现不佳，高斯属性训练不充分，快速移动物体经常从重建中丢失，因此需要一种更鲁棒的时空建模方式。

Method: 提出时空位置隐式网络SPIN-4DGS，从显式收集的时空位置学习高斯属性，而非建模时间位移；采用轻量前馈网络预测所有时空位置的高斯属性，避免显式优化的巨大内存开销；基于光栅化重建损失进行训练，捕获共享表示以保持时空一致性。

Result: 在CMU Panoptic数据集的挑战性运动场景中，SPIN-4DGS在PSNR和SSIM上均优于现有方法，例如在Basketball场景中比最强基线D3DGS高出1.83 PSNR。

Conclusion: SPIN-4DGS通过从时空位置直接学习而非建模位移，有效提升了4D高斯抛雪球在快速运动和大位移场景下的重建质量与稳定性。

Original Abstract: Recent 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) methods often fail under fast motion with large inter-frame displacements, where Gaussian attributes are poorly learned during training, and fast-moving objects are often lost from the reconstruction. In this work, we introduce Spatiotemporal Position Implicit Network for 4DGS, coined SPIN-4DGS, which learns Gaussian attributes from explicitly collected spatiotemporal positions rather than modeling temporal displacements, thereby enabling more faithful splatting under fast motions with large inter-frame displacements. To avoid the heavy memory overhead of explicitly optimizing attributes across all spatiotemporal positions, we instead predict them with a lightweight feed-forward network trained under a rasterization-based reconstruction loss. Consequently, SPIN-4DGS learns shared representations across Gaussians, effectively capturing spatiotemporal consistency and enabling stable high-quality Gaussian splatting even under challenging motions. Across extensive experiments, SPIN-4DGS consistently achieves higher fidelity under large displacements, with clear improvements in PSNR and SSIM on challenging sports scenes from the CMU Panoptic dataset. For example, SPIN-4DGS notably outperforms the strongest baseline, D3DGS, by achieving +1.83 higher PSNR on the Basketball scene.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的4D高斯抛雪球（4DGS）方法在快速运动和大帧间位移的场景下常常失败，此时高斯属性在训练过程中难以充分学习，快速移动的物体往往从重建结果中丢失。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种用于4DGS的时空位置隐式网络，即SPIN-4DGS，该方法从显式收集的时空位置中学习高斯属性，而非对时间位移进行建模，从而在大帧间位移的快速运动下实现更保真的抛雪球渲染。为了避免对所有时空位置的属性进行显式优化所带来的沉重内存开销，我们转而利用一个轻量级的前馈网络来预测这些属性，该网络在基于光栅化的重建损失下进行训练。因此，SPIN-4DGS能够学习高斯之间的共享表示，有效捕捉时空一致性，即便在具有挑战性的运动场景下也能实现稳定且高质量的高斯抛雪球渲染。通过大量实验，SPIN-4DGS在大位移场景下始终获得更高的保真度，在CMU Panoptic数据集的挑战性体育场景中，PSNR和SSIM均取得明显提升。例如，在Basketball场景中，SPIN-4DGS显著优于最强基线D3DGS，PSNR提高了1.83。

</details>


### [37] [Lost in Visual Translation: A VLM-Assisted Perceptual-Semantic Coherence Framework for EEG-to-Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12364)
*Sukriti Tiwari,BHVSP Subrahmanyam,Nidhi Goyal,Sai Amrit Patnaik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对脑电信号到图像重建（EEG-to-image）评估中现有指标（SSIM、LPIPS、CLIP）无法区分视觉保真度与可恢复语义的问题，提出了一套脑机接口（BCI）感知的评估框架，包含T-PAS、T-SAS和蒸馏后的BCS评分，能够更可靠地衡量重建图像的语义可恢复性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像质量评估指标（SSIM、LPIPS、CLIP）应用于EEG重建图像时存在问题：像素级指标与语义一致性几乎零相关，表示级指标混淆了感知错误和语义错误，无法正确区分视觉保真度与可恢复语义，导致对模糊、扭曲但语义可恢复的重建图像给出不合理评分。

Method: 提出BCI感知评估框架：1) 使用四个视觉语言模型（VLMs）通过结构化问题评估图像对，产生容忍感知对齐分数（T-PAS）和容忍语义对齐分数（T-SAS）；2) 将VLM共识蒸馏为紧凑评估器——BCI一致性分数（BCS），该评估器在T-PAS上MAE为0.079（r=0.700），T-SAS上MAE为0.082（r=0.850）。

Result: 分析了来自ATM、ENIGMA、BrainVis和DreamDiffusion的6,855组真实/重建图像对，发现像素级指标与语义一致性几乎零相关；人类验证显示联合一致性判断高度可靠（Cohen's kappa = 0.882±0.174, Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882）。

Conclusion: BCS框架优于传统指标，支持以感知-语义可恢复性而非通用视觉相似性来评估EEG重建图像，为BCI领域提供了更合理的评估标准。

Original Abstract: EEG-to-image evaluation should distinguish visual fidelity from recoverable meaning. Yet EEG-derived reconstructions are blurry, distorted, and low-detail, causing SSIM, LPIPS, and CLIP to penalize semantically recoverable outputs or reward plausible but incorrect ones. We analyze 6,855 ground-truth/reconstruction pairs from ATM, ENIGMA, BrainVis, and DreamDiffusion using semantic probes, caption harshness and blind-spot rates, and controlled degradations. Pixel metrics show near-zero correlation with semantic consistency, while representation metrics conflate perceptual and semantic errors. We therefore introduce a BCI-aware framework in which four VLMs assess image pairs through structured questions, producing Tolerant Perceptual Alignment Scores (T-PAS) and Tolerant Semantic Alignment Scores (T-SAS). Their consensus is distilled into the BCI-Coherence Score (BCS), a compact evaluator achieving a T-PAS MAE of 0.079 (r = 0.700) and a T-SAS MAE of 0.082 (r = 0.850) on our data. Human validation shows highly reliable joint coherence judgments, with Cohen's kappa = 0.882 +/- 0.174 and Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882, supporting perceptual-semantic recoverability over generic visual similarity. Code and resources are available at https://sukt03.github.io/BCS/.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电解码图像（EEG-to-image）评估应区分视觉保真度与可恢复语义。然而，EEG重建图像通常模糊、扭曲且细节不足，导致SSIM、LPIPS和CLIP等指标要么惩罚语义可恢复的输出，要么奖励看似合理但不正确的重建。我们使用语义探针、字幕严苛度与盲点率分析以及受控退化实验，分析了来自ATM、ENIGMA、BrainVis和DreamDiffusion的6,855组真实/重建图像对。结果表明，像素级指标与语义一致性几乎零相关，而表示级指标则混淆了感知错误与语义错误。为此，我们引入了一个脑机接口（BCI）感知评估框架，其中四个视觉语言模型通过结构化问题对图像对进行评估，产生容忍感知对齐分数（T-PAS）和容忍语义对齐分数（T-SAS）。将这些模型的共识蒸馏为BCI一致性分数（BCS），这是一个紧凑的评估器，在我们的数据上实现了T-PAS平均绝对误差（MAE）为0.079（r=0.700）和T-SAS MAE为0.082（r=0.850）。人类验证显示联合一致性判断高度可靠，Cohen's kappa = 0.882 ± 0.174，Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882，支持以感知-语义可恢复性而非通用视觉相似性作为评估标准。代码和资源可在https://sukt03.github.io/BCS/获取。

</details>


### [38] [UMSS: Towards Unsupervised Multi-modal Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12372)
*Haitian Zhang,Thai Duy Nguyen,Xiangyuan Wang,Mohan Liu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出无监督多模态语义分割（UMSS）任务，并构建了UniM²框架，通过跨模态对应协同（CMCS）学习统一潜在空间，以及跨模态协调器（CMH）抑制不一致关系监督，在无需标签的情况下有效利用多模态互补信息，在NYU Depth v2和MFNet数据集上分别提升mIoU 6.4%和9.8%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态语义分割在复杂环境中至关重要，但人工标注成本过高。现有无监督语义分割仅在单模态RGB上表现良好，直接扩展到多模态数据时，由于缺乏显式监督，模型难以协调不同传感器捕获的异构结构模式，导致融合退化，无法有效利用互补信息。

Method: 提出UniM²框架，基于DINOv3构建。核心包括：（1）跨模态对应协同（CMCS），学习统一潜在空间以提取内在共享语义线索；（2）跨模态协调器（CMH），以RGB为稳定参考，抑制不一致的关系监督，同时引导模型利用互补结构特征。

Result: 在NYU Depth v2数据集上mIoU提升6.4%，在MFNet数据集上mIoU提升9.8%，显著优于现有UMSS框架。

Conclusion: 本文首次探索无监督多模态语义分割问题，提出的UniM²框架通过统一潜在空间学习和跨模态协调机制，有效解决了多模态融合退化问题，在完全无标签条件下实现了显著的性能提升。

Original Abstract: Multimodal semantic segmentation (MSS) is essential for robust perception in complex environments, yet its potential remains largely untapped because of the prohibitive cost of human annotations. While unsupervised semantic segmentation (USS) has achieved strong results on a single RGB modality, its naive extension to multimodal data is often hindered by fusion degradation. This occurs because, without explicit supervision, existing frameworks struggle to reconcile the heterogeneous structural patterns captured by different sensors and therefore fail to effectively exploit their complementary information. In this paper, we make the first attempt to address the novel problem of Unsupervised Multimodal Semantic Segmentation (UMSS), aiming to effectively exploit complementary sensor information in a fully label free setting. To this end, we propose UniM2 (Unified Multimodal), a novel framework built on DINOv3 that transforms conventional fusion methods into consistent performance gains. Our key idea is to learn a unified latent space driven by Cross Modal Correspondence Synergy (CMCS) to extract intrinsic shared semantic cues, bypassing the need for label guided adaptive fusion. To mitigate inherent intermodal conflicts, we introduce a Cross Modal Harmonizer (CMH) that designates RGB as a stable reference, effectively suppressing inconsistent relational supervision while guiding the model to exploit complementary structural features. Extensive experimental results on NYU Depth v2 and MFNet show that UniM2 improves mIoU by 6.4% and 9.8%, respectively, demonstrating clear advantages over existing frameworks for UMSS.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态语义分割（MSS）对于复杂环境中的鲁棒感知至关重要，但由于人工标注的高昂成本，其潜力仍未得到充分挖掘。虽然无监督语义分割（USS）已在单一RGB模态上取得了优异结果，但其直接扩展到多模态数据时常受融合退化问题的阻碍。这是因为在缺乏显式监督的情况下，现有框架难以协调不同传感器捕获的异构结构模式，从而无法有效利用其互补信息。本文首次尝试解决无监督多模态语义分割（UMSS）这一新问题，旨在完全无标签的条件下有效利用互补的传感器信息。为此，我们提出了UniM²（统一多模态）框架，该框架基于DINOv3构建，将传统融合方法转化为一致性的性能提升。我们的核心思想是学习一个由跨模态对应协同（CMCS）驱动的统一潜在空间，以提取内在共享语义线索，从而绕过标签引导的自适应融合需求。为缓解固有的模态间冲突，我们引入了跨模态协调器（CMH），将RGB指定为稳定参考，有效抑制不一致的关系监督，同时引导模型利用互补的结构特征。在NYU Depth v2和MFNet上的大量实验结果表明，UniM²的mIoU分别提升了6.4%和9.8%，在UMSS任务上展现出相较于现有框架的明显优势。

</details>


### [39] [Demonstration of the common dual-channel feature decoupling characteristic of front-door mediation causal inference methods in whole-slice image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12376)
*Zhirui Zhang,Tianhang Nan,Yong Ding,Zhuolun Song,Dayu Hu,Xiaoyu Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了两个用于评估全切片图像（WSI）分析中因果推断多实例学习（MIL）方法的假设，为消除虚假关联提供了新的理论视角。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果推断MIL方法通过调整证据子图像的特征分布来建立正确关联，但缺乏对其工作机制的理论解释和评估标准。

Method: 提出并证明两个假设：1) 因果推断MIL引入独立分类通道有效完成WSI分类；2) 新通道与基线通道提取的特征差异越大，消除虚假关联的效果越好。基于这两个假设对多种因果推断MIL方法进行评估。

Result: 在乳腺癌和非小细胞肺癌数据集上评估了多种因果推断MIL方法，验证了所提假设的有效性。

Conclusion: 该假设为将因果推断应用于WSI分析提供了新的理论视角，揭示了通过增加深度特征多样性来消除虚假关联的核心机制。

Original Abstract: Causal inference using front door intervention and multi-instance learning (MIL) has advanced the analysis of Whole Slide Images (WSI) in digital pathology. These methods adjust feature distributions of subtle evidence sub-images to correctly associate them with WSI-level diagnoses. We propose and prove 2 hypotheses for evaluating such methods: 1) Causal inference MIL introduces an independent classification channel that effectively completes WSI classification; 2) Greater difference between features extracted by the new and baseline channels increases effectiveness in eliminating false correlations. This hypothesis describes the core of causal inference MILs: overlaying parallel, independent channels to eliminate false associations between WSI-level diagnostic and non-diagnostic evidence sub-images by increasing deep feature diversity. Based on these hypotheses, we evaluated several causal inference MILs on breast cancer and non-small cell lung cancer datasets. This hypothesis provides a new theoretical perspective for applying causal inference to WSI analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 利用前门干预和多实例学习（MIL）的因果推断方法推动了数字病理学中全切片图像（WSI）分析的发展。这些方法调整细微证据子图像的特征分布，以正确地将它们与WSI级别的诊断相关联。我们提出并证明了评估此类方法的两个假设：1) 因果推断MIL引入了一个独立的分类通道，有效完成WSI分类；2) 新通道与基线通道提取的特征之间的差异越大，消除虚假关联的效果越好。该假设描述了因果推断MIL的核心机制：叠加平行的独立通道，通过增加深度特征多样性来消除WSI级别诊断证据子图像与非诊断证据子图像之间的虚假关联。基于这些假设，我们在乳腺癌和非小细胞肺癌数据集上评估了多种因果推断MIL方法。该假设为将因果推断应用于WSI分析提供了新的理论视角。

</details>


### [40] [SeamGen: Artist-Aligned UV Seam Generation via Graph Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12379)
*Hao Xu,Yuqing Zhang,Yiqian Wu,Xueqi Ma,Ding Liang,Yan-Pei Cao,Ying-Tian Liu,Xiaogang Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeamGen，一个基于流匹配生成模型的UV接缝生成方法，通过学习专业艺术家接缝布局数据的分布，利用网格原生Transformer架构替代传统的手工设计目标，生成更符合艺术家偏好和生产需求的UV布局。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动UV接缝放置方法主要依赖基于目标的逐对象优化（如手工设计的失真避免目标或预训练模型的语义代理），但这些策略与工业流水线中艺术家偏好的接缝模式和生产需求不一致，生成结果偏离实际应用要求。

Method: 提出SeamGen，采用流匹配生成模型学习大量现有接缝布局中每条边接缝标签的分布。核心创新是Mesh Transformer骨干网络，它交替进行网格边上的局部图注意力与跨顶点的全局自注意力，捕获细粒度几何线索和长程拓扑连贯性。此外，利用流模型的免训练修复能力实现局部接缝优化和约束引导的接缝生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，通过学习专业接缝布局数据的先验，SeamGen生成的UV布局相比基于失真和语义代理的基线方法，更符合艺术家偏好，感知质量更优。

Conclusion: SeamGen通过生成式方法学习艺术家偏好，替代手工设计目标，能够生成更符合工业流水线要求的UV接缝布局，在感知质量上优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: UV seam placement is a critical yet labor-intensive step in 3D content creation, requiring artists to balance chart shape, seam concealment, and alignment with semantic and geometric features. Existing automatic methods are primarily based on per-object optimization, relying on handcrafted objectives to avoid distortion or on proxies from pretrained models to inject semantic information. However, these strategies are not always well aligned with seams used in industrial production pipelines, often resulting in layouts that deviate from artist-preferred seam patterns and practical production requirements. To address these limitations, we propose SeamGen, a generative model for UV seam generation that aligns with artist preferences and production requirements. Instead of depending on manually designed objectives and constraints, SeamGen learns the distribution of per-edge seam labels from a large corpus of existing seam layouts using a flow-matching generative model. A key challenge is that typical Transformer architectures used in flow matching models are designed for sequential representations, such as point clouds, and cannot naturally account for mesh topology. To enable mesh-native learning, we design a Mesh Transformer backbone that interleaves local graph attention over mesh edges with global self-attention across vertices, capturing both fine-grained geometric cues and long-range topological coherence. To further improve inference-time controllability and quality, we exploit the training-free inpainting capability of flow models for both localized seam refinement and constraint-guided seam generation. Extensive experiments show that by learning priors from professional seam layout data, SeamGen produces UV layouts that better align with artist-authored preferences and achieve superior perceptual quality compared with distortion-based and semantic-proxy baselines.

中文摘要翻译: UV接缝放置是3D内容创作中一个关键但劳动密集的步骤，要求艺术家在图表形状、接缝隐藏以及与语义和几何特征对齐之间取得平衡。现有的自动方法主要基于逐对象优化，依赖手工设计的目标来避免失真，或依赖预训练模型的代理来注入语义信息。然而，这些策略并不总是与工业流水线中使用的接缝对齐，常常导致布局偏离艺术家偏好的接缝模式和实际生产要求。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了SeamGen，一个用于UV接缝生成的生成模型，能够与艺术家偏好和生产要求对齐。SeamGen不依赖手工设计的目标和约束，而是使用流匹配生成模型从大量现有接缝布局中学习每条边接缝标签的分布。一个关键挑战在于，流匹配模型中使用的典型Transformer架构是为序列表示（如点云）设计的，无法自然地考虑网格拓扑。为了实现网格原生学习，我们设计了一个Mesh Transformer骨干网络，它在网格边上的局部图注意力与跨顶点的全局自注意力之间交替进行，同时捕获细粒度的几何线索和长程拓扑连贯性。为了进一步提高推理时的可控性和质量，我们利用流模型的免训练修复能力进行局部接缝细化和约束引导的接缝生成。大量实验表明，通过学习专业接缝布局数据的先验，SeamGen生成的UV布局比基于失真和语义代理的基线方法更符合艺术家偏好的模式，并实现更优的感知质量。

</details>


### [41] [Seeing Globally, Refining Locally: Global Visual Guidance and Local Ultrasound Cues for Robust Freehand 3-D Ultrasound Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12398)
*Yameng Zhang,Zhongyu Chen,Dianye Huang,Xiangyu Chu,K. W. Samuel Au,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种全局到局部的手持式三维超声姿态估计框架，融合双摄像头全局定位与B超图像局部精细调整，有效抑制长扫描轨迹中的累积漂移误差，在体模和体内数据集上显著优于纯超声和纯双摄像头基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无跟踪器的手持式三维超声重建方法在长扫描轨迹中容易受到累积姿态误差的影响，导致重建精度下降，亟需一种能同时保证全局一致性和局部精细化的姿态估计方法。

Method: 提出US + Dual-Cam全局到局部姿态估计框架：(1) 双摄像头分支跨视角和时序进行上下文特征聚合，估计全局一致的探头轨迹；(2) B模式分支从连续超声图像中聚合解剖特征，捕捉组织相关的局部运动线索；(3) 跨模态融合模块整合摄像头与超声特征，在变换空间中预测姿态残差以精细调整；(4) 多尺度姿态损失在多个时间跨度上约束相对运动，抑制长时间扫描中的累积漂移。

Result: 在两个体内数据集上，US + Dual-Cam模型将平均轨迹漂移降至1.67 mm和1.29 mm，相比强双摄像头基线分别提升16.50%和27.12%，远优于纯超声姿态估计（>13 mm漂移）。在体前臂动脉重建中Hausdorff距离为1.58 mm。

Conclusion: 提出的跨模态融合框架有效结合了外部摄像头的全局定位能力和超声图像的局部解剖信息，显著抑制了长扫描轨迹中的累积漂移，在真实临床场景中展现了良好的重建精度和实用性。

Original Abstract: Freehand 3-D ultrasound (US) imaging has attracted increasing attention owing to its intuitive volumetric visualization, ease of use, and low cost. However, accurate 3-D reconstruction critically depends on stable probe pose estimation, yet existing trackerless methods remain susceptible to accumulated pose errors, particularly over long scanning trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose a global-to-local pose estimation framework that exploits external camera observations for globally stable localization and B-mode US images for anatomy-aware local refinement. Specifically, the framework comprises a dual-camera branch that performs contextual feature aggregation across camera views and temporal observations to estimate a globally consistent probe trajectory, and a B-mode branch that performs anatomical feature aggregation from sequential US images to capture tissue-dependent local motion cues. A cross-modal fusion module subsequently integrates the contextual camera features and anatomical US features to predict pose residuals and refine the camera-derived estimates in the transformation space. Furthermore, a multi-scale pose loss constrains relative motion over multiple temporal horizons to suppress accumulated drift during extended scans. The proposed framework is validated on phantom and in vivo datasets. On two in-house datasets (FUSION-J and FUSION-L) collected using different machines, the proposed US + Dual-Cam model reduces average trajectory drift to 1.67 mm and 1.29 mm, representing improvement of 16.50% and 27.12%, respectively, over a strong dual-camera baseline, while substantially outperforming US-only pose estimation (>13 mm drift). In in vivo forearm arteries reconstruction, it achieves Hausdorff distances of 1.58 mm, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method on real clinical scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 手持式三维超声成像因其直观的体积可视化、易用性和低成本而受到越来越多的关注。然而，精确的三维重建关键依赖于稳定的探头姿态估计，但现有的无跟踪器方法仍然容易受到累积姿态误差的影响，特别是在长扫描轨迹中。为了解决这一局限，我们提出了一种全局到局部的姿态估计框架，该框架利用外部摄像头观测实现全局稳定的定位，并利用B模式超声图像实现解剖感知的局部精细化。具体而言，该框架包含一个双摄像头分支，该分支跨摄像头视角和时间观测进行上下文特征聚合，以估计全局一致的探头轨迹；以及一个B模式分支，该分支从序列超声图像中进行解剖特征聚合，以捕捉组织相关的局部运动线索。随后，一个跨模态融合模块整合上下文摄像头特征和解剖超声特征，在变换空间中预测姿态残差并精细化摄像头估计的结果。此外，多尺度姿态损失在多个时间跨度上约束相对运动，以抑制长时间扫描中的累积漂移。所提出的框架在体模和体内数据集上进行了验证。在使用不同机器采集的两个内部数据集（FUSION-J和FUSION-L）上，提出的US + Dual-Cam模型将平均轨迹漂移降低至1.67 mm和1.29 mm，相比强双摄像头基线分别提升了16.50%和27.12%，同时显著优于纯超声姿态估计（>13 mm漂移）。在体内前臂动脉重建中，该方法实现了1.58 mm的Hausdorff距离，证明了所提方法在真实临床场景中的有效性。

</details>


### [42] [Physically Aware Radiomics Without Interpolation: Disentangling Voxel Geometry and Signal Modification in CT and MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12399)
*David Corral Fontecha,Juan Miranda Bautista,Pablo Menendez Fernández-Miranda,Sergio Rubio-Martín,Lara Lloret Iglesias,Jose A. Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种体素间距感知的影像组学框架，在不重采样的前提下将物理几何信息融入纹理计算，解决了各向异性图像中传统方法混淆体素几何与插值信号变化的问题。实验表明该方法在保持原始信号和预测性能的同时，比各向同性重采样具有更好的一致性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统影像组学纹理特征在体素索引邻域中计算，隐式假设空间关系各向同性。在各向异性图像中，这会将体素几何与插值导致的信号变化相混淆，影响特征可靠性和可重复性。

Method: 修改PyRadiomics以考虑体素间距同时保留原始图像信号，对比四种配置：原生非重采样提取(NR)、各向同性重采样(RS)、体素间距感知提取(VS)和伪各向同性预处理(FK，仅改写间距元数据而不改变图像数组)。在685个LIDC-IDRI肺结节和209个I-SPY2乳腺MRI病例上评估196个影像组学描述符。

Result: VS与NR高度一致：CT中位ICC(A,1)=0.9976，MRI中为0.9984。RS一致性较低且偏差更大，FK表现居中，证实间距元数据本身会影响影像组学特征。梯度导出和邻域敏感的描述符受预处理影响最大。VS在外部CT验证中保持与NR相当的预测性能，而MRI在不同预处理策略和分类器间表现出更大变异性。

Conclusion: 体素间距感知提取将几何建模与插值导致的信号修改分离开来，同时保留原始图像信号，为各向异性CT和MRI的影像组学分析提供了各向同性重采样的一个连贯替代方案。

Original Abstract: Objective: Radiomic texture features are usually computed in voxel-index neighborhoods, implicitly assuming isotropic spatial relationships. In anisotropic images, this can confound voxel geometry with interpolation-induced signal changes. We developed a voxel-spacing-aware radiomic framework that incorporates physical geometry into texture computation without resampling.
  Approach: We modified PyRadiomics to account for voxel spacing while preserving the native image signal. Four configurations were compared: native non-resampled extraction (NR), isotropic resampling (RS), voxel-spacing-aware extraction (VS), and fake-isotropic preprocessing (FK), in which spacing metadata were overwritten without altering the image array. Experiments included 685 LIDC-IDRI pulmonary nodules and 209 I-SPY2 breast MRI cases, with 196 radiomic descriptors. Robustness was assessed using ICC, within-subject variability, Friedman testing, feature selection, machine learning, a multilayer perceptron, and external validation.
  Main results: VS showed near-native agreement with NR: median ICC(A,1) was 0.9976 in CT and 0.9984 in MRI. RS produced lower agreement and larger deviations, while FK showed intermediate behavior, confirming that spacing metadata alone can affect radiomic features. Gradient-derived and neighborhood-sensitive descriptors were most affected by preprocessing. VS preserved predictive performance comparable to NR in external CT validation, whereas MRI showed greater variability across preprocessing strategies and classifiers.
  Significance: Voxel-spacing-aware extraction separates geometric modeling from interpolation-induced signal modification while preserving the native image signal, offering a coherent alternative to isotropic resampling for radiomic analysis of anisotropic CT and MRI.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：影像组学纹理特征通常在体素索引邻域中计算，隐式假设空间关系各向同性。在各向异性图像中，这会将体素几何与插值引起的信号变化相混淆。我们开发了一种体素间距感知的影像组学框架，在不重采样的前提下将物理几何信息融入纹理计算。方法：我们修改了PyRadiomics以考虑体素间距，同时保留原始图像信号。比较了四种配置：原生非重采样提取(NR)、各向同性重采样(RS)、体素间距感知提取(VS)和伪各向同性预处理(FK，仅改写间距元数据而不改变图像数组)。实验包括685个LIDC-IDRI肺结节和209个I-SPY2乳腺MRI病例，使用196个影像组学描述符。采用ICC、组内变异、Friedman检验、特征选择、机器学习、多层感知机和外部验证评估鲁棒性。主要结果：VS与NR高度一致：CT中位ICC(A,1)为0.9976，MRI为0.9984。RS一致性较低且偏差更大，FK表现居中，证实间距元数据本身会影响影像组学特征。梯度导出和邻域敏感的描述符受预处理影响最大。VS在外部CT验证中保持与NR相当的预测性能，而MRI在不同预处理策略和分类器间表现出更大变异性。意义：体素间距感知提取将几何建模与插值引起的信号修改分离开来，同时保留原始图像信号，为各向异性CT和MRI的影像组学分析提供了各向同性重采样的一个连贯替代方案。

</details>


### [43] [Contrastive-Augmented Flow Matching for Style-Content Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12404)
*Yusong Li,Pingchuan Ma,Ming Gui,Vincent Tao Hu,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAtFM通过将对比正则化集成到可逆流匹配框架中，在CLIP等嵌入空间上实现内容与风格表征的解耦，提升了检索性能、聚类分离度和开放集鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流模型主要使用生成目标训练，往往导致内容与风格因素纠缠或未对齐，难以实现可控生成和组合泛化。

Method: 提出对比增强流匹配（CAtFM），在可逆流匹配中引入对比正则化，对预测端点施加对比监督以强制跨传输分布的语义一致性，使解耦隐式涌现，无需假设严格纯化或完全分解的内容与风格表征。

Result: 在合成数据、域内风格以及ImageNet、WikiArt、DomainNet、DTD等真实基准上，CAtFM在内容和风格检索、嵌入聚类分离度和开放集鲁棒性方面均优于生成式和判别式基线。

Conclusion: CAtFM提供了一种简单有效的方式，将判别式约束与确定性传输相结合，在分布偏移下改善了解耦效果和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Learning representations that separate content and style is crucial for controllable generation and compositional generalization. However, diffusion and flow-based models trained primarily with generative objectives often produce entangled or misaligned factors. To address this gap, we introduce Contrastive Augmented Flow Matching (CAtFM), a framework that integrates contrastive regularization into an invertible flow matching formulation to promote structured content-style representations. Rather than constraining intermediate latents or velocity fields, we apply contrastive supervision to predicted endpoints during training, enforcing semantic consistency across transported distributions while allowing disentanglement to emerge implicitly, without assuming strictly pure or fully factorized content and style representations. Our main experiments operate in the CLIP embedding space, with additional validation using frozen DINO and ALIGN encoders. Across synthetic data, in-domain styles, and real-world benchmarks (ImageNet, WikiArt, DomainNet, and DTD), CAtFM improves content and style retrieval, enhances embedding cluster separation, and achieves stronger open-set robustness compared to generative and discriminative baselines. Overall, CAtFM provides a simple way to couple discriminative constraints with deterministic transport, improving disentanglement and robustness under distribution shift.

中文摘要翻译: 学习分离内容与风格的表征对于可控生成和组合泛化至关重要。然而，主要以生成目标训练的扩散模型和流模型往往产生纠缠或未对齐的因素。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了对比增强流匹配（CAtFM），该框架将对比正则化集成到可逆流匹配公式中，以促进结构化的内容-风格表征。我们并非约束中间潜变量或速度场，而是在训练期间对预测端点施加对比监督，强制跨传输分布的语义一致性，同时允许解耦隐式涌现，无需假设严格纯化或完全分解的内容与风格表征。我们的主要实验在CLIP嵌入空间中进行，并使用冻结的DINO和ALIGN编码器进行额外验证。在合成数据、域内风格以及真实世界基准（ImageNet、WikiArt、DomainNet和DTD）上，CAtFM在内容与风格检索、增强嵌入聚类分离度方面均有提升，并且与生成式和判别式基线相比，实现了更强的开放集鲁棒性。总体而言，CAtFM提供了一种简单的方法，将判别式约束与确定性传输相结合，在分布偏移下改善了解耦效果和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [44] [Virtual Chromoendscopy with Tunable Visibility Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12416)
*Yuhi Kanno,Yusuke Monno,Sho Suzuki,Tomohiro Tada,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟增强染色内镜（V-ECE），通过结合图像翻译与增强技术，并引入用户可调节的增强水平，在统一模型中生成不同增强程度的虚拟染色内镜图像，以提升胃部病变可见性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统染色内镜（CE）虽然能有效检测胃部病变，但染料准备和喷洒过程增加了额外成本和时间；现有虚拟染色内镜（V-CE）虽能通过图像翻译避免此问题，但无法根据病变类型或医生偏好灵活调整增强程度。

Method: 提出一种新颖的图像翻译模型，该模型能够以用户可调节的增强水平参数为条件，从标准内镜（SE）图像生成虚拟增强染色内镜（V-ECE）图像，在统一模型中实现不同增强程度的可控生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的模型能够使用统一模型合理地生成具有不同增强水平的V-ECE图像。

Conclusion: 提出的V-ECE方法可在统一模型框架下，通过用户可调的增强水平参数，灵活生成不同增强程度的虚拟染色内镜图像，有效提升胃部病变可见性，同时避免传统CE的额外成本和时间开销。

Original Abstract: Chromoendoscopy (CE) is a common clinical practice that sprays indigo carmine blue dye onto the gastric surface to improve the visibility of gastric lesions, such as an early cancer. While CE is effective in detecting the lesions, preparing and spraying the dye needs additional cost and time, which is undesirable both for patients and medical practitioners. To overcome this issue, virtual chromoendoscopy (V-CE) was recently proposed, which applies a learned image translation model to virtually generate a CE image from a standard endoscopy (SE) image. In this paper, we propose virtual enhanced chromoendoscopy (V-ECE) that combines V-CE with image enhancement techniques to further improve the visibility of gastric lesions. Because a desired enhancement level depends on the inspected lesion and the practitioner's preference, we introduce a novel image translation model that can generate V-ECE images using an enhancement level tunable by a user. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed model can plausibly generate V-ECE images with various enhancement levels using a unified model.

中文摘要翻译: 染色内镜（CE）是一种常见的临床实践，通过将靛蓝胭脂红染料喷洒在胃表面来改善胃部病变（如早期癌症）的可视性。虽然CE在检测病变方面有效，但制备和喷洒染料需要额外的成本和时间，这对患者和医务人员来说都是不期望的。为了解决这个问题，近期提出了虚拟染色内镜（V-CE），它应用学习到的图像翻译模型，从标准内镜（SE）图像虚拟生成CE图像。在本文中，我们提出了虚拟增强染色内镜（V-ECE），它将V-CE与图像增强技术相结合，以进一步提高胃部病变的可视性。由于期望的增强水平取决于所检查的病变和医生的偏好，我们引入了一种新颖的图像翻译模型，该模型能够以用户可调节的增强水平生成V-ECE图像。实验结果表明，我们提出的模型能够使用统一模型合理地生成具有不同增强水平的V-ECE图像。

</details>


### [45] [MQAdapter: Multi-Modal Quantum Adapter for Coarse-to-Fine VLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12418)
*Yumiao Zhao,Bo Jiang,Min Lu,Xiao Wang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子计算的粗到细多模态适配器（MQAdapter），用于提升视觉-语言模型在少样本细粒度分类中的Top-1准确率。MQAdapter通过量子纠缠和叠加在希尔伯特空间中建模高阶跨模态交互，产生更具判别性的特征表示，在15个数据集上优于现有方法且参数效率更高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在少样本分类中虽然能通过候选类别过滤获得高Top-K准确率，但在视觉相似类别的细粒度区分上表现不佳（Top-1性能差）。现有适配器方法仅关注全局对齐，未能利用语义相似的类别来细化细粒度视觉表示。

Method: MQAdapter首先检索与输入图像最相似的Top-K类别候选作为语义锚点，然后通过跨模态量子学习机制，将视觉和文本特征编码为量子态，利用量子纠缠和叠加在希尔伯特空间中建模高阶跨模态交互，从而在锚点指导下细化视觉特征，产生更具判别性的表示。

Result: 在15个数据集上的评估表明，MQAdapter在需要更少可训练参数的情况下取得了优于现有方法的性能，并且可以集成到多种微调算法中进一步提升效果。

Conclusion: MQAdapter是一种参数高效的粗到细VLM微调方法，通过量子计算有效提升了少样本细粒度分类性能，具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language Models have demonstrated impressive transfer learning capabilities across a wide range of tasks. For few-shot classification, we observe that VLMs exhibit a notable ability to filter candidate categories and thus achieve high Top-K accuracy. However, they often struggle with fine-grained discrimination among visually similar categories, resulting in unsatisfactory Top-1 performance, as shown in Figure 1. Existing studies on VLM adapters generally focus on global alignment between visual and textual representations in the feature space, but fail to exploit semantically similar categories to refine fine-grained visual representations. Based on these observations, we propose a novel coarse-to-fine VLM fine-tuning approach for few-shot learning that leverages quantum computation, termed the Multi-Modal Quantum Adapter (MQAdapter). Specifically, MQAdapter first retrieves the Top-K category candidates most similar to the input image and uses them as semantic anchors. It then employs a cross-modal quantum learning mechanism to refine visual features under the guidance of these anchors. The core of this mechanism is the encoding of visual and textual features into quantum states. By leveraging quantum entanglement and superposition in a high-dimensional Hilbert space, MQAdapter effectively models higher-order cross-modal interactions, producing more discriminative representations than traditional Euclidean adapters. MQAdapter is parameter-efficient and can be integrated with various existing fine-tuning algorithms to achieve further performance gains. Evaluations on 15 datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of MQAdapter while requiring fewer trainable parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模视觉-语言模型在广泛的任务中展现了出色的迁移学习能力。在少样本分类任务中，我们观察到VLM具有显著的候选类别过滤能力，从而获得高Top-K准确率。然而，它们通常在视觉相似类别之间的细粒度区分上表现不佳，导致Top-1性能不理想，如图1所示。现有的VLM适配器研究通常关注特征空间中视觉和文本表示之间的全局对齐，但未能利用语义相似的类别来细化细粒度视觉表示。基于这些观察，我们提出了一种新颖的、基于量子计算的粗到细VLM微调方法用于少样本学习，称为多模态量子适配器（MQAdapter）。具体而言，MQAdapter首先检索与输入图像最相似的Top-K候选类别作为语义锚点，然后在这些锚点的指导下，利用跨模态量子学习机制来细化视觉特征。该机制的核心是将视觉和文本特征编码为量子态。通过利用高维希尔伯特空间中的量子纠缠和叠加，MQAdapter有效地建模了高阶跨模态交互，产生比传统欧几里得适配器更具判别性的表示。MQAdapter参数高效，可集成到多种现有微调算法中进一步提升性能。在15个数据集上的评估证明了MQAdapter的有效性，且所需可训练参数更少。

</details>


### [46] [DeGuNet: Depth-Guided Ultra-Compact Backbones for Efficient LiDAR-Camera 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12419)
*Haifa Zhang,Yijing Wang,Peixi Peng,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeGuNet，一个超紧凑、即插即用的图像骨干网络，专为自动驾驶中激光雷达与相机的多模态3D目标检测设计。它通过深度引导的稀疏感知特征提取，消除传统2D视觉骨干的参数冗余和结构错位，在nuScenes数据集上实现高达66.5%的GPU内存节省、1.16倍推理加速和最高6.20 mAP的绝对提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前多模态3D目标检测框架严重依赖在2D语义任务上预训练的大规模视觉骨干网络，这带来了巨大的参数冗余和结构错位——2D先验无法有效处理鸟瞰图几何中激光雷达投影的极端稀疏性。

Method: DeGuNet是一个超紧凑的即插即用图像骨干网络，专为深度引导的表示学习设计。它引入稀疏感知特征提取机制，有效对齐多视角图像与非结构化的激光雷达深度，同时严格防止无效区域污染。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，DeGuNet具有广泛的即插即用适用性。集成到现有基线后，GPU内存消耗降低高达66.5%，推理速度提升1.16倍，同时实现最高6.20的绝对mAP增益。

Conclusion: DeGuNet建立了一种参数高效的多模态3D感知新范式，从根本上消除了架构冗余，为自动驾驶感知中的多模态融合提供了更高效、更精准的解决方案。

Original Abstract: In autonomous driving perception, the fusion of LiDAR and camera modalities has become the dominant paradigm for 3D object detection. However, current multi-modal frameworks heavily rely on massive visual backbones pretrained on 2D semantic tasks. This reliance introduces substantial parameter redundancy and a structural misalignment, as 2D priors are ill-equipped to handle the extreme sparsity of LiDAR projections required for Bird's-Eye-View geometry. To address this, we present DeGuNet, an ultra-compact and plug-and-play image backbone explicitly designed for depth-guided representation learning. By incorporating sparsity-aware feature extraction mechanisms, DeGuNet effectively aligns multi-view images with unstructured LiDAR depth while strictly preventing invalid-region contamination. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate DeGuNet's broad plug-and-play applicability and superior efficiency. When integrated into established baselines, it fundamentally eliminates architectural redundancy, reducing GPU memory consumption by up to 66.5% and achieving a 1.16x inference speedup. Concurrently, DeGuNet delivers up to a 6.20 absolute mAP gain, establishing a new paradigm for parameter-efficient multi-modal 3D perception.

中文摘要翻译: 在自动驾驶感知中，激光雷达与相机模态的融合已成为3D目标检测的主导范式。然而，当前的多模态框架严重依赖于在2D语义任务上预训练的大规模视觉骨干网络。这种依赖引入了大量的参数冗余和结构错位，因为2D先验无法有效处理鸟瞰图几何中所需的激光雷达投影的极端稀疏性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了DeGuNet——一个超紧凑且即插即用的图像骨干网络，专为深度引导的表示学习而设计。通过引入稀疏感知特征提取机制，DeGuNet有效对齐多视角图像与非结构化的激光雷达深度，同时严格防止无效区域污染。在nuScenes数据集上的大量实验表明，DeGuNet具有广泛的即插即用适用性和卓越的效率。当集成到现有基线中时，它从根本上消除了架构冗余，将GPU内存消耗降低高达66.5%，并实现1.16倍的推理加速。同时，DeGuNet取得了最高6.20的绝对mAP增益，为参数高效的多模态3D感知建立了新的范式。

</details>


### [47] [More Than Where You Are: Learning Semantics, Structure, and Geometry from Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12429)
*Mao Chen,Xiangkai Zhang,Zhiyong Liu,Chuankai Liu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CROSS框架，将跨视角定位从传统位姿估计重新定义为统一的跨视角理解任务，通过3D接地对齐、结构感知匹配和假设排序，实现了极端视角变化下的稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何，在KITTI和VIGOR数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有跨视角定位方法存在三个关键局限性：缺乏显式3D接地、依赖严格的逐点匹配削弱语义一致性、以及基于绝对目标函数学习导致几何推理指导不足。作者认为跨视角定位不应仅被视为2D匹配或位姿估计，而应帮助模型在极端视角变化下发展一致的跨视角理解能力，包括稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何。

Method: CROSS框架包含三个核心模块：1) 3D接地对齐（3D-grounded alignment），将2D特征显式投影到3D空间进行对齐；2) 结构感知匹配（structure-aware matching），在匹配过程中保持语义一致性；3) 假设排序（hypothesis ranking），通过排序学习为几何推理提供更有效的监督信号。这三个模块使结构学习成为内在需求、鼓励语义表征保持稳定、并使模型获得可迁移的几何知识。

Result: 在KITTI和VIGOR数据集上的大量实验表明，CROSS在跨视角定位任务上达到了最先进的性能。更重要的是，CROSS能够有效学习到极端视角变化下的稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何。

Conclusion: 跨视角定位不应被简化为位姿估计问题，而应被视为发展一致跨视角理解能力的途径。通过3D接地对齐、结构感知匹配和假设排序，CROSS框架成功实现了极端视角变化下的语义稳定性、结构可靠性和几何可迁移性，为空间智能中的跨视角理解提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Consistent cross-view understanding under extreme viewpoint changes is essential for spatial intelligence, as it enables models to recognize the same scene across extreme viewpoint gaps. Cross-view localization naturally provides a promising pathway toward this ability, as it requires a model to align ground-view imagery with geo-referenced satellite-view imagery despite drastic appearance changes to estimate camera poses. Recent visual foundation models have made this long-standing localization problem increasingly feasible by providing rich 2D representations for cross-view matching. However, we argue that cross-view localization should not be viewed merely as 2D matching or pose estimation. In this work, we revisit cross-view localization as more than pose estimation and investigate how it can help the model develop consistent cross-view understanding under extreme viewpoint changes, including stable semantics, reliable structure, and transferable geometry. We identify three key limitations of existing methods that prevent them from achieving this. They usually lack explicit 3D grounding, rely on strict point-wise matching that can weaken semantic consistency, and learn from an absolute objective that provides limited guidance for geometric reasoning. To address these limitations, we propose CROSS, a unified cross-view localization framework built upon 3D-grounded alignment, structure-aware matching, and hypothesis ranking. This formulation makes structure learning an intrinsic requirement, encourages semantic representations to remain stable, and enables the model to acquire transferable geometry. Extensive experiments on the KITTI and VIGOR datasets show that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-view localization. More importantly, CROSS effectively learns stable semantics, reliable structure, and transferable geometry across extremely different viewpoints.

中文摘要翻译: 极端视角变化下的一致跨视角理解对于空间智能至关重要，因为它使模型能够在巨大的视角差异下识别同一场景。跨视角定位自然为实现这一能力提供了有前景的途径，因为它要求模型在剧烈外观变化的情况下，将地面视角图像与地理参考的卫星视角图像对齐以估计相机位姿。最近的视觉基础模型通过提供丰富的2D表示用于跨视角匹配，使得这一长期存在的定位问题日益可行。然而，我们认为跨视角定位不应仅仅被视为2D匹配或位姿估计。在这项工作中，我们重新审视跨视角定位，将其视为超越位姿估计的任务，并研究它如何帮助模型在极端视角变化下发展一致的跨视角理解能力，包括稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何。我们指出现有方法在实现这一目标方面存在的三个关键局限性：它们通常缺乏显式3D接地、依赖可能削弱语义一致性的严格逐点匹配、以及从为目标函数学习而提供的几何推理指导有限。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出CROSS，一个基于3D接地对齐、结构感知匹配和假设排序的统一跨视角定位框架。这一公式使结构学习成为内在需求，鼓励语义表征保持稳定，并使模型能够获得可迁移几何。在KITTI和VIGOR数据集上的大量实验表明，CROSS在跨视角定位中达到了最先进的性能。更重要的是，CROSS在极端视角差异下有效学习了稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何。

</details>


### [48] [ARDepth: Auto-regressive Monocular Depth Estimation with Progressive Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12433)
*Zijie Wang,Wei Zhang,Weiming Zhang,Xiao Tan,Weikai Chen,Xiaoxu Li,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARDepth，将单目深度估计建模为结构化自回归生成过程，通过逐尺度渐进构建深度表示，替代传统的全局去噪范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型将深度恢复视为全局平滑场的迭代去噪，未能显式反映场景几何的**分段平滑**和**尺度依赖**特性。实际上，几何结构在不同空间尺度上渐进涌现（粗布局→表面→边界），现有方法缺乏对这种层次化构建过程的显式建模。

Method: 1. **自回归生成框架**：随空间分辨率增加，逐步构建深度表示，而非全局细化；2. **尺度渐进条件控制（SPC）**：在每个生成阶段注入多尺度视觉特征；3. **语义感知引导（SAG）**：提供场景级语义先验，增强全局结构一致性。

Result: 实验表明该方法在深度估计任务上表现强劲，能产生跨尺度结构一致的深度预测，验证了自回归生成作为几何建模新范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 自回归生成是一种有前景的替代范式，能够更好地捕捉细粒度局部细节同时保持全局几何一致性，尤其适用于需要显式层次化几何建模的场景。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models have recently become the dominant paradigm for monocular depth estimation (MDE). However, they implicitly assume that depth can be recovered as a globally smooth field through iterative denoising, which does not explicitly reflect the piecewise and scale-dependent organization of scene geometry. In practice, geometric structure emerges progressively across spatial scales, where coarse layout, surfaces, and boundaries are constructed in a hierarchical manner. Motivated by this observation, we introduce ARDepth, which formulates depth estimation as structured auto-regressive generation. Instead of recovering depth through global refinement, ARDepth progressively constructs depth representations as spatial resolution increases. To support this generative process, we introduce Scale-Progressive Conditioning (SPC) to inject multi-scale visual features at each generation stage, and Semantic-Aware Guidance (SAG) to provide scene-level semantic priors that enhance global structural consistency. Together, these designs enable the model to capture fine-grained local details while maintaining coherent global geometry. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance and produces structurally consistent depth predictions across scales, validating auto-regressive generation as a promising alternative paradigm for geometric modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型近期已成为单目深度估计（MDE）的主流范式。然而，这些方法隐式假设深度可通过迭代去噪恢复为一个全局平滑场，未能显式反映场景几何的**分段式**和**尺度依赖**的组织方式。实际上，几何结构在空间尺度上渐进涌现：粗粒度布局、表面和边界以层次化方式构建。受此观察启发，我们提出ARDepth，将深度估计建模为**结构化自回归生成**。ARDepth并非通过全局细化恢复深度，而是随着空间分辨率增加逐步构建深度表示。为支撑这一生成过程，我们引入**尺度渐进条件控制（SPC）**，在每个生成阶段注入多尺度视觉特征；以及**语义感知引导（SAG）**，提供场景级语义先验以增强全局结构一致性。这些设计共同使模型能够捕获细粒度局部细节，同时保持连贯的全局几何。实验结果表明，该方法取得了强劲性能，并生成了跨尺度结构一致的深度预测，验证了自回归生成作为几何建模的一种有前景的替代范式。

</details>


### [49] [Let RGB Be the Language of Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12450)
*Timing Yang,Jinrui Yang,Xinlong Li,Yuhan Wang,Haoran Li,Yanqing Liu,Guoyizhe Wei,Jixuan Ying,Chen Wei,Rama Chellappa,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RINO框架，将各种视觉信息（掩码、深度图等）统一表示为RGB图像，并将视觉任务转化为RGB到RGB的图像编辑问题，从而实现单一模型跨任务零样本泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉模型通常针对特定任务或特定输入输出格式进行设计，缺乏像语言模型那样统一的表达和处理范式。作者希望统一视觉信息的表示形式（统一为RGB），使不同视觉任务能够通过共享的视觉接口进行交互和迁移。

Method: 提出RGB In and RGB Out（RINO）框架，将所有结构化视觉信号（掩码、深度图、姿态等）都编码为RGB图像形式，将各类视觉任务统一转化为RGB到RGB的图像编辑问题。模型基于通用图像编辑骨干网络，无需任务特定的微调，实现零样本跨任务迁移。

Result: 在密集理解任务（分割、深度估计）和密集条件生成任务（姿态到图像生成）上，RINO展示了鲁棒且具有竞争力的零样本性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建统一的视觉-语言系统提供了有益思路，表明多样的视觉任务可以通过共享的视觉语言来表达、解释和求解。

Original Abstract: This work introduces a unified formulation for vision models, where diverse forms of visual information beyond natural images, such as masks, depth maps, and other structured visual signals, are all represented as RGB images, while general visual tasks can be converted into a common RGB-to-RGB image editing problem. In this paradigm, different types of visual information internally share the same encoding and decoding architecture and parameters as natural images, enabling a single model to transfer across tasks through a unified visual interface, in a way analogous to how language models operate over text. We refer to this formulation as RGB In and RGB Out (RINO). Built upon a generic image editing backbone without task-specific fine-tuning, RINO demonstrates robust and competitive zero-shot performance on both dense understanding tasks such as segmentation and depth estimation (where we unify outputs as RGB), and dense-conditioned generation tasks such as pose-to-image generation (where we unify inputs as RGB). We hope this study provides useful insights toward general unified vision-language systems, where diverse visual tasks can be expressed, interpreted, and solved through a shared visual language. Code is available at https://github.com/yangtiming/RINO.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种统一的视觉模型形式化方法，其中，除自然图像之外的各种视觉信息形式，如掩码、深度图及其他结构化视觉信号，均被表示为RGB图像，而一般的视觉任务则可转化为一个通用的RGB到RGB图像编辑问题。在该范式下，不同类型的视觉信息在内部与自然图像共享相同的编码器和解码器架构及参数，从而使单一模型能够通过统一的视觉接口跨任务迁移，类似于语言模型在文本上的运作方式。我们将这种形式化方法称为RGB In and RGB Out（RINO）。RINO基于通用图像编辑骨干网络构建，无需任务特定的微调，在分割与深度估计等密集理解任务（我们将输出统一为RGB）以及姿态到图像生成等密集条件生成任务（我们将输入统一为RGB）上均展现出鲁棒且具有竞争力的零样本性能。我们希望这项研究能为构建通用的统一视觉-语言系统提供有益启示，使多样化的视觉任务能够通过共享的视觉语言得以表达、解释和求解。代码已开源：https://github.com/yangtiming/RINO。

</details>


### [50] [Steering Diffusion Models via Class-Contrastive Influence for Few-Shot Medical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12464)
*Jeeyung Kim,Erfan Esmaeili,Qiang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出类对比影响（C2I）标准，通过梯度影响量化样本对分类器的有用性，并利用强化学习微调扩散模型生成高C2I样本，以提升少样本医学图像分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型数据增强方法主要关注生成样本的真实性、多样性和域适应，但忽略了如何度量和优化生成样本对下游分类任务的有用性这一根本问题。

Method: 提出类对比影响（C2I）标准，通过样本损失梯度与同类验证集梯度的对齐程度以及与异类梯度的对抗程度来衡量样本有用性；进而以C2I为奖励，利用强化学习微调扩散模型，引导其生成具有高C2I的类别信息丰富样本。

Result: 在多个少样本医学影像基准上，C2I引导的生成相比现有扩散增强基线方法显著提升了下游分类的准确率和鲁棒性；分析表明高C2I样本是位于决策边界附近的困难样本，有助于优化决策边界。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强的最有效方式应以任务有用性而非图像质量本身为导向，C2I标准为此提供了一种可量化和可优化的途径。

Original Abstract: When labeled data are scarce, off-the-shelf diffusion models can augment training sets for few-shot medical image classification, but not all generated samples are equally useful for the downstream task. Existing approaches largely improve synthetic data by increasing realism, diversity, or domain adaptation, while overlooking a more fundamental question: how should sample usefulness for classification be measured and optimized? We address this with Class-Contrastive Influence (C2I), a criterion that quantifies a sample's usefulness through its gradient-based influence on the classifier. We find that effective samples exhibit a strong C2I gap: their loss gradients align with validation gradients from the same class and oppose those from other classes. Our analysis further suggests that such high-C2I samples are hard, boundary-proximal examples that help refine the decision boundary and improve robustness. Building on this insight, we fine-tune diffusion models with reinforcement learning using a C2I-based reward to steer generation toward class-informative samples. Across several few-shot medical imaging benchmarks, C2I-guided generation improves downstream accuracy and robustness over diffusion-based augmentation baselines, showing that synthetic augmentation is most effective when guided by task usefulness rather than image quality alone.

中文摘要翻译: 当标注数据稀缺时，现成的扩散模型可以为少样本医学图像分类扩充训练集，但并非所有生成样本对下游任务同样有用。现有方法主要通过提升真实性、多样性或域适应来改进合成数据，却忽视了一个更根本的问题：如何度量和优化样本对分类的有用性？本文提出类对比影响（C2I）准则，通过样本基于梯度的分类器影响来量化其有用性。我们发现有效样本表现出强烈的C2I差距：其损失梯度与同类验证梯度对齐，并与异类梯度相对抗。进一步分析表明，这类高C2I样本是位于决策边界附近的困难样本，有助于优化决策边界并提升鲁棒性。基于这一发现，我们利用基于C2I的奖励通过强化学习微调扩散模型，引导生成朝向类别信息丰富的样本。在多个少样本医学影像基准上，C2I引导的生成相比基于扩散的增强基线方法提升了下游分类的准确率和鲁棒性，表明合成数据增强在任务有用性而非仅图像质量指导下最为有效。

</details>


### [51] [Self in Space: Benchmarking Self-Awareness and Spatial Cognition in UAV Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12477)
*Zhishan Zou,Guoyan Sun,Zhiwei Wei,Jiancheng Pan,Yujie Li,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIS-Bench基准，用于评估无人机场景下 embodied 空间智能的自我感知能力，发现当前MLLMs在动态和以自我为中心的过程中存在根本性局限，并提出运动感知表示方法以融合自我相关动态信息，显著提升感知与记忆性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无人机方法和基准主要关注环境中心的空间理解任务，而智能体的自我感知（self-awareness）仍停留在隐式层面。为了解决这一空白，需要统一的自我在空间中（self-in-space）的评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建SIS-Bench基准，包含4,856个问答对、13个任务、1,646个真实无人机视频，沿空间与自我两个维度以及感知、记忆、推理三个认知层级组织评估。2) 提出运动感知表示（motion-aware representation），通过光流与视觉特征融合来建模自我相关的动态信息。

Result: 当前MLLMs在建模动态和以自我为中心的过程中存在根本局限；空间认知与自我意识之间存在明显不平衡；认知层级越高，性能逐渐退化；运动感知表示能持续提升感知和记忆性能，并泛化到下游无人机决策任务。

Conclusion: 自我感知对于推进具身空间智能至关重要，本文提供了新的基准和运动感知自我在空间建模的经验证据。

Original Abstract: Autonomous UAV systems increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to operate in complex real-world environments. Such embodied scenarios require not only understanding the surrounding space but also maintaining a coherent representation of the agent itself. However, existing UAV-oriented approaches and benchmarks remain largely environment-centric, primarily focusing on spatial understanding tasks, with the agent's self-awareness remaining implicit. To address this gap, we introduce SIS-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence in UAV scenarios under a unified self-in-space formulation. SIS-Bench organizes evaluation along two complementary dimensions, space and self, and a three-level hierarchy of perception, memory, and reasoning. It contains 4,856 question--answer pairs across 13 tasks derived from 1,646 real-world UAV videos through a task-conditioned construction pipeline with expert verification.Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs exhibit fundamental limitations in modeling dynamic and agent-centered processes. In particular, we observe a clear imbalance between spatial cognition and self-awareness, as well as a progressive performance degradation across cognitive levels.Motivated by these findings, we further explore a motion-aware representation that incorporates self-related dynamics through optical flow and visual feature fusion. Experimental results show that modeling agent motion consistently improves perception and memory performance, not only in spatial cognition but also in self-awareness, and generalizes to downstream UAV decision-making tasks.Our results highlight the importance of self-awareness for advancing embodied spatial intelligence, and provide both a new benchmark and empirical evidence for motion-aware self-in-space modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 自主无人机系统越来越依赖多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在复杂的现实环境中运行。这种具身场景不仅需要理解周围空间，还需要维持智能体自身的一致表征。然而，现有面向无人机的方法和基准仍然以环境为中心，主要关注空间理解任务，而智能体的自我感知仍停留在隐式层面。为解决这一空白，我们引入SIS-Bench，一个在统一的"自我在空间中"（self-in-space）公式下评估无人机场景中具身空间智能的基准。SIS-Bench沿两个互补维度——空间与自我，以及感知、记忆与推理三个层级来组织评估。它包含来自1,646个真实无人机视频的13个任务的4,856个问答对，通过任务条件化的构建流程和专家验证生成。广泛的评估表明，当前MLLMs在建模动态和以自我为中心的过程中存在根本性局限。特别是，我们观察到空间认知与自我意识之间的明显不平衡，以及跨认知层级的渐进式性能退化。受这些发现启发，我们进一步探索了一种运动感知表示，通过光流与视觉特征融合来融合自我相关的动态信息。实验结果表明，建模智能体运动持续提升了感知和记忆性能——不仅在空间认知方面，也在自我意识方面——并能泛化到下游无人机决策任务。我们的结果凸显了自我感知对于推进具身空间智能的重要性，并为运动感知的"自我在空间中"建模提供了新的基准和经验证据。

</details>


### [52] [RealSkin: Spatio-Spectral Partial Neural Adjoint Maps for Image-to-3D Attribute Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12495)
*Jing Li,Yawei Luo,Xiangze Meng,Ying Li,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealSkin 提出了一种自监督框架，通过在学习的谱域中进行对应优化，解决了从真实图像到合成3D表面的外观迁移问题，在真实到合成场景中达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法受限于分辨率不匹配和点对应的离散性问题，而基于功能图的方法虽能实现平滑属性传播，但依赖近等距假设和拓扑一致性。RealSkin旨在突破这些限制，处理严重的拓扑差异和非等距对应。

Method: 首先提出空间引导配准算法建立粗对应；然后设计谱感知神经伴随网络，将部分对应纳入神经函数空间，并建模非等距残差以精化对应。

Result: 在具有挑战的真实到合成场景中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: RealSkin有效解决了真实到合成外观迁移中的拓扑差异和非等距问题，实现了高质量的光照真实感3D资产生成。

Original Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D assets requires bridging the appearance gap between real-world observations and synthetic models. A promising approach is to transfer visual attributes from real images onto synthetic 3D surfaces. Traditional methods struggle with resolution mismatch and the inherent discreteness of point correspondences. In contrast, resolution-robust functional maps enable smooth attribute propagation but rely on near-isometry assumptions and topological consistency. To address these limitations, we propose RealSkin, a self-supervised framework that performs correspondence optimization in a learned spectral domain, guided by spatial correspondences. We first introduce a spatial-guided registration algorithm to establish coarse correspondences under severe topological discrepancies. To relax strict isometric assumptions and handle partial correspondences, we further design a spectral-aware neural adjoint network that incorporates partial correspondences into a neural function space and models non-isometric residuals for correspondence refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging real-to-synthetic scenarios. The code will be publicly released.

中文摘要翻译: 创建逼真的3D资产需要弥合真实世界观察与合成模型之间的外观差距。一种有前景的方法是将真实图像的视觉属性迁移到合成3D表面上。传统方法难以处理分辨率不匹配以及点对应的固有不连续性。相比之下，分辨率鲁棒的功能图能够实现平滑的属性传播，但依赖于近等距假设和拓扑一致性。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了RealSkin——一个自监督框架，在由空间对应引导的学习谱域中执行对应优化。我们首先引入一种空间引导的配准算法，在严重的拓扑差异下建立粗对应。为了放宽严格的等距假设并处理部分对应问题，我们进一步设计了一个谱感知神经伴随网络，将部分对应纳入神经函数空间，并建模非等距残差以进行对应精化。实验结果表明，我们的方法在具有挑战性的真实到合成场景中达到了最先进的性能。代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [53] [TerraLogic: A Benchmark for Hierarchical Geospatial Reasoning in Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12497)
*Yuhang Yan,Linchao Mou,Bokang Yang,Qingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TerraLogic基准和HieraPlan方法，用于推动遥感领域从感知层面向认知层面的地理空间推理，实验证明现有方法在层级推理上表现不足，而HieraPlan提供了强基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感研究主要聚焦于感知导向任务，忽视了认知层面的地理空间推理，亟需一个系统化的基准和方法来推进该方向。

Method: 提出TerraLogic基准（545个层级化场景驱动任务，涵盖光学、SAR、红外影像）和HieraPlan工具增强智能体（将工具包组织为功能层级，具备容错推理能力）。

Result: 当前方法在层级化地理空间推理上表现困难；HieraPlan在推理能力、跨模态泛化和错误处理方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: TerraLogic可作为地理空间推理的标准评测平台，HieraPlan为复杂遥感分析任务提供了有效的方案，数据集和代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Beyond perception, reasoning is essential in remote sensing for advanced interpretation, inference, and decision-making. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled tool-augmented agents that leverage external tools to perform complex analytical tasks. However, existing studies in remote sensing primarily focus on perception-oriented tasks, leaving cognitive geospatial reasoning largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce TerraLogic, a benchmark for geospatial reasoning. TerraLogic comprises 545 scenario-driven, hierarchy-aware tasks, such as hazard vulnerability assessment, urban heat island analysis, and forest fragmentation dynamics, spanning optical, Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR), and infrared (IR) imagery. It advances evaluation beyond recognition and monitoring toward cognitive-level geospatial analysis. To facilitate evaluation on TerraLogic, we further propose HieraPlan, a tool-augmented agent that organizes toolkits into functional hierarchies and performs fault-tolerant reasoning. HieraPlan enables structured abstraction, robust recovery from tool failures, and stable long-horizon planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that current approaches struggle with hierarchical geospatial reasoning, while HieraPlan provides a strong baseline with improved reasoning, cross-modal generalization, and error handling. The dataset and agent code are publicly available at https://github.com/Ireliya/TerraLogic.

中文摘要翻译: 超越感知层面，推理在遥感的高级解译、推断与决策中至关重要。大语言模型的最新进展催生了工具增强型智能体，可利用外部工具执行复杂的分析任务。然而，现有遥感研究主要聚焦于感知导向任务，认知层面的地理空间推理仍远未得到充分探索。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了TerraLogic——一个地理空间推理基准。TerraLogic包含545个场景驱动、层级化感知的任务，例如灾害脆弱性评估、城市热岛分析和森林破碎化动态，涵盖光学、合成孔径雷达（SAR）和红外（IR）影像。它将评估从识别与监控提升至认知层面的地理空间分析。为便于在TerraLogic上进行评估，我们进一步提出了HieraPlan，一种工具增强型智能体，它将工具包组织为功能层级并执行容错推理。HieraPlan实现了结构化抽象、工具故障下的稳健恢复以及稳定的长程规划。大量实验表明，当前方法在层级化地理空间推理上表现不佳，而HieraPlan提供了强有力的基线，在推理能力、跨模态泛化和错误处理方面均有提升。数据集和智能体代码已在https://github.com/Ireliya/TerraLogic公开。

</details>


### [54] [DynTrace: Tracking Dynamic Object Evidence for 4D Spatio-Temporal Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12503)
*Rongxin Gao,Yuzhi Huang,Dongxuan Liu,Chu Li,Zhenye Wang,Jie Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Xinghao Ding,Xiaotong Tu,Yue Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynTrace，一种无需训练的四维时空推理框架，通过动态轨迹可视化（DTV）和动态轨迹标记（DT-Token）两个互补组件，为多模态大语言模型提供连续追踪的动态物体证据，在多个基准上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在静态场景理解和粗粒度4D任务上表现良好，但在连续动态场景感知方面存在显著局限，主要依赖稀疏帧级观测，无法区分物体真实运动与相机引起的表观运动，导致连续动态线索被割裂。

Method: 提出DynTrace框架，包含两个互补组件：(1) 动态轨迹可视化（DTV），将世界坐标轨迹重投影到图像平面，提供几何信息引导的视觉先验，解耦物体真实运动与相机表观运动；(2) 动态轨迹标记（DT-Token），组织为动态轨迹图（DTG），追踪物体级动态线索、轨迹演化和关键时刻，维持连续动态物体证据。

Result: 在Dyn-Bench、VLM4D和DSI-Bench三个基准上均取得最先进结果，一致提升了开源MLLMs的性能。

Conclusion: 追踪动态物体证据对于稳健的4D时空推理至关重要，DynTrace通过几何引导的视觉先验和结构化时空轨迹有效解决了这一问题。

Original Abstract: 4D spatio-temporal reasoning, jointly modeling 3D spatial structure and temporal evolution, is essential for understanding dynamic worlds and enabling embodied interaction. While current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong capabilities in static scene understanding and coarse-grained 4D tasks, they still have notable limitations in continuous dynamic scene perception, especially in tracking dynamic object evidence for coherent 4D spatio-temporal reasoning. This shortcoming stems mainly from relying on sparse frame-level observations, fragmenting continuous dynamic cues and leaving models unable to disentangle genuine object dynamics from camera-induced apparent motion. Inspired by humans tracking dynamic cues while compensating for viewpoint changes, we propose DynTrace, a training-free framework for 4D spatio-temporal reasoning with two complementary components. Dynamic Trajectory Visualization (DTV) reprojects world-coordinate trajectories onto the image plane, providing geometry-informed visual priors that disentangle genuine object dynamics from camera-induced apparent motion. Meanwhile, the Dynamic Trace Token (DT-Token), organized into a Dynamic Trace Graph (DTG), tracks object-level dynamic cues, trace evolution, and key moments, maintaining continuous dynamic object evidence for coherent 4D reasoning. Together, these two components equip MLLMs with continuously tracked dynamic object evidence, grounded in geometry-informed visual priors and structured spatio-temporal traces. DynTrace consistently improves open-source MLLMs, achieving state-of-the-art results on Dyn-Bench, VLM4D, and DSI-Bench, validating the importance of tracking dynamic object evidence for robust 4D spatio-temporal reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 四维时空推理——联合建模3D空间结构与时间演化——对于理解动态世界和实现具身交互至关重要。尽管当前的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在静态场景理解和粗粒度4D任务上展现出强大能力，但在连续动态场景感知方面仍然存在显著局限，尤其是在追踪动态物体证据以实现连贯的4D时空推理方面。这一不足主要源于依赖稀疏的帧级观测，导致连续动态线索被割裂，使模型无法区分物体的真实运动与相机引起的表观运动。受人类在补偿视角变化的同时追踪动态线索的启发，我们提出了DynTrace，一个无需训练的四维时空推理框架，包含两个互补组件。动态轨迹可视化（DTV）将世界坐标轨迹重投影到图像平面，提供几何信息引导的视觉先验，从而解耦物体真实运动与相机表观运动。同时，动态轨迹标记（DT-Token）被组织为动态轨迹图（DTG），追踪物体级动态线索、轨迹演化和关键时刻，维持连续动态物体证据以支持连贯的4D推理。这两个组件共同为MLLMs提供基于几何信息引导的视觉先验和结构化时空轨迹的连续追踪动态物体证据。DynTrace一致提升了开源MLLMs的性能，在Dyn-Bench、VLM4D和DSI-Bench上取得了最先进的结果，验证了追踪动态物体证据对于稳健的四维时空推理的重要性。

</details>


### [55] [DiTailed: Ensuring Visual Object Consistency in Text-Image-to-Image Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12539)
*Francesco Taioli,Daniel Coelho,Iaroslav Melekhov,Roberto Alcover-Couso,Jose Miguel Grande Saiz,Virginia Fernandez Arguedas,Artur Bekasov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本引导图像编辑中的视觉对象一致性问题，构建了ABO-Edit数据集，揭示了图像编辑整流流模型中条件嵌入空间可预测最终生成图像的特性，并提出无需额外参数的辅助损失FlowMirror以提升编辑质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本引导图像生成模型在编辑过程中常常无法保持目标对象的关键属性（即视觉对象一致性），缺乏专门针对该问题的数据集和有效的解决方案。

Method: 首先构建了包含12,000+三元组（源图像、编辑提示、目标图像）的高质量数据集ABO-Edit；其次发现图像编辑整流流模型的条件嵌入空间即使在高噪声水平下也能编码最终生成图像的预测信息；基于此提出FlowMirror，一种无需参数修改的辅助损失函数，对该条件嵌入空间进行监督。

Result: 在无架构变化的情况下，FlowMirror在多项评估指标上相较于基线方法显著提升了图像编辑的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过对条件嵌入空间的监督，可以有效改善编辑过程中的对象一致性，该方法简单高效且无需改变模型架构。

Original Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in text-guided image editing, generative models frequently fail to preserve visual object consistency, defined as the preservation of a subject's key attributes throughout the editing process. We address this limitation through three contributions. First, we introduce ABO-Edit, a dataset specifically designed to study object consistency, comprising over 12,000 triplets of source images, editing prompts, and high-quality target images rendered from artist-designed 3D assets, with multi-view coverage and human-verified quality control. Second, we uncover an overlooked property of image-editing rectified flow models: the conditioning embedding space, not directly supervised during training, encodes a prediction of the final generated image even at high noise levels. Third, exploiting this finding, we propose FlowMirror, a parameter-free auxiliary loss that supervises this conditioning embedding space. Without architectural changes, our method improves generation quality across several metrics over baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管在文本引导的图像编辑方面取得了显著进展，生成模型在编辑过程中常常无法保持视觉对象一致性，即目标的关键属性在编辑过程中得以保留。我们通过三项贡献来解决这一局限性。首先，我们引入了ABO-Edit，一个专门为研究对象一致性而设计的数据集，包含超过12,000个三元组，每个三元组由源图像、编辑提示以及由艺术家设计的3D资产渲染得到的高质量目标图像组成，具有多视图覆盖和人工验证的质量控制。其次，我们揭示了图像编辑整流流模型中一个被忽视的特性：条件嵌入空间在训练过程中未受到直接监督，但即使在高噪声水平下也能编码最终生成图像的预测信息。第三，基于这一发现，我们提出了FlowMirror，一种无需额外参数的辅助损失函数，对该条件嵌入空间进行监督。无需架构改动，我们的方法在多项指标上相较于基线方法提升了生成质量。

</details>


### [56] [Edge-Aware Thermal Infrared UAV Swarm Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12544)
*Yu-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向边缘部署的自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF）在线跟踪流水线，在保持实时效率的同时，通过状态相关运动学建模、瞬态误报抑制和运动学驱动的预测巡航，提升了热红外条件下微型无人机跟踪的轨迹连续性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无人机跟踪方法主要关注跟踪精度，忽视了实时边缘部署的计算约束；标准卡尔曼滤波虽适合边缘设备，但其匀速假设在无人机高动态运动和热传感器抖动下容易失效；而非线性估计器虽能提升鲁棒性，但会引入额外计算开销。

Method: 提出自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF），在标准线性卡尔曼滤波基础上引入状态相关的运动学建模，同时结合瞬态误报抑制和运动学驱动的预测巡航机制，构成完整的边缘感知在线跟踪流水线。

Result: 在Beyond Strong Baseline（BSB）基准上进行了实验，联合评估了跟踪性能和计算效率，为未来实时边缘部署提供了见解。

Conclusion: AKKF在保持实时边缘部署效率的同时，提升了热红外条件下无人机跟踪的轨迹连续性，为边缘感知的无人机跟踪提供了新的研究起点。

Original Abstract: Thermal infrared (TIR) imaging is essential for UAV swarm operations in visually degraded environments. However, tracking tiny UAVs remains challenging due to limited appearance cues, frequent occlusions, and rapid maneuvers. Despite significant progress driven by benchmarks such as the Anti-UAV challenge, existing methods primarily prioritize accuracy while overlooking the computational constraints of real-time edge deployment. The standard Kalman Filter (KF) offers the efficiency required for edge devices, yet its constant-velocity assumption often breaks down under highly dynamic UAV motion and thermal sensor jitter. More sophisticated nonlinear estimators can improve robustness but often introduce additional computational costs. To address this gap, we propose an edge-aware online tracking pipeline centered on the Adaptive Kinematic Kalman Filter (AKKF), which augments the linear KF with state-dependent kinematic modeling while preserving real-time efficiency. Combined with transient false-positive suppression and kinematics-driven predictive coasting, the presented pipeline improves trajectory continuity under challenging TIR conditions. Experiments on the Beyond Strong Baseline (BSB) benchmark provide a starting point for edge-aware UAV tracking by jointly evaluating tracking performance and computational efficiency, offering insights toward future real-time deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 热红外（TIR）成像对于视觉退化环境下的无人机集群任务至关重要。然而，由于外观线索有限、频繁遮挡以及快速机动，跟踪微型无人机仍然具有挑战性。尽管Anti-UAV挑战赛等基准推动了显著进展，现有方法主要优先考虑精度，却忽略了实时边缘部署的计算约束。标准卡尔曼滤波（KF）具备边缘设备所需的效率，但其匀速假设在高动态无人机运动和热传感器抖动下经常失效。更复杂的非线性估计器能够提升鲁棒性，但往往引入额外的计算开销。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种以自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF）为核心的边缘感知在线跟踪流水线，该滤波在保持实时效率的同时，通过状态相关的运动学建模增强了线性KF。结合瞬态误报抑制和运动学驱动的预测巡航，所提出的流水线在具有挑战性的TIR条件下提升了轨迹连续性。在Beyond Strong Baseline（BSB）基准上的实验通过联合评估跟踪性能和计算效率，为边缘感知的无人机跟踪提供了研究起点，并为未来实时部署提供了见解。

</details>


### [57] [CGRL: Concept-Guided Pruning and Representation Learning for Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12556)
*Thuc Huynh,Tuan Le,Doanh C. Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出概念引导的剪枝与表示学习框架（CGRL），通过引入疾病提示导出的类别级概念原型，在弱监督全切片图像分类中对补丁进行概念相关性剪枝和对比表示学习，提升了MIL方法的性能并降低了计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有弱监督全切片图像分类中的多实例学习方法主要依赖视觉线索聚合大量补丁嵌入，会保留许多非信息性补丁，且实例特征与类别级疾病语义之间的对齐较弱。

Method: 首先通过概念相关性剪枝，根据补丁实例与类别概念的相似度排序并保留top-K个概念相关补丁用于下游MIL聚合；其次通过概念引导的对比表示学习，从同一相似度矩阵构建类别正负补丁集，优化目标类、对称辅助和跨类分离三个对比目标，正则化投影后的概念空间。

Result: 在TCGA-BRCA和TCGA-NSCLC数据集上使用多种代表性MIL方法进行实验，CGRL在多个模型-数据集组合上均有提升，在准确率和宏F1上取得了明显改进，同时通过概念相关性剪枝降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 类别级语义概念为弱监督计算病理学中的补丁选择和表示学习提供了有效且实用的先验知识。

Original Abstract: Weakly supervised whole-slide image (WSI) classification is widely used in computational pathology because slide-level labels are easier to obtain than dense region annotations. Existing multiple instance learning (MIL) methods often aggregate large bags of patch embeddings using mainly visual cues, which can retain many non-informative patches and provide weak alignment between instance features and class-level disease semantics. We propose Concept-Guided Pruning and Representation Learning (CGRL), a simple framework that introduces class-level concept prototypes derived from disease prompts into the MIL pipeline. First, concept-relevance pruning ranks patch instances by their similarity to class concepts and retains the top-K concept-relevant patches for downstream MIL aggregation. Second, concept-guided contrastive representation learning constructs class-wise positive and negative patch sets from the same similarity matrix and optimizes target-class, symmetric auxiliary, and cross-class separation objectives, thereby regularizing the projected concept space. We evaluate CGRL on TCGA-BRCA and TCGA-NSCLC using multiple representative MIL methods. Experimental results show that CGRL improves several model-dataset combinations, with gains depending on the downstream MIL model and dataset. It achieves particularly clear improvements in accuracy and macro-F1 while reducing computational cost through concept-relevance pruning. These findings demonstrate that class-level semantic concepts provide an effective and practical prior for patch selection and representation learning in weakly supervised computational pathology.

中文摘要翻译: 弱监督全切片图像分类在计算病理学中被广泛使用，因为切片级标签比密集的区域标注更容易获得。现有的多实例学习方法通常主要利用视觉线索来聚合大量补丁嵌入，这可能会保留许多非信息性补丁，并导致实例特征与类别级疾病语义之间的对齐较弱。我们提出了概念引导的剪枝与表示学习（CGRL），这是一个简单的框架，将疾病提示导出的类别级概念原型引入MIL流程。首先，概念相关性剪枝根据补丁实例与类别概念的相似度对其进行排序，并保留top-K个概念相关补丁用于下游MIL聚合。其次，概念引导的对比表示学习从同一相似度矩阵中构建类别级的正负补丁集，并优化目标类、对称辅助和跨类分离三个对比目标，从而对投影后的概念空间进行正则化。我们在TCGA-BRCA和TCGA-NSCLC数据集上使用多种代表性MIL方法评估了CGRL。实验结果表明，CGRL改进了多个模型-数据集组合，其收益取决于下游MIL模型和数据集。它在准确率和宏F1上取得了明显改进，同时通过概念相关性剪枝降低了计算成本。这些发现表明，类别级语义概念为弱监督计算病理学中的补丁选择和表示学习提供了有效且实用的先验知识。

</details>


### [58] [Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Event-Aware Visual Allocation in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12557)
*Yifan Lu,Ziqi Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Jun Gao,Bing Li,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV
